Start ROS/Gazebo/PX4/XRCE environment.\\n2. UGV appears.\\n3. UAVs are associated with deployment slots.\\n4. UGV reaches deployment point.\\n5. Deployment is triggered.\\n6. PX4 vehicle becomes ready.\\n7. UAV arms and takes off.\\n8. UAV performs physical search.\\n9. Gazebo wildlife enters camera view.\\n10. Camera publishes image.\\n11. detector processes real image.\\n12. Detection has defensible map/world position.\\n13. tracker creates persistent Target.\\n14. additional UAV detection fuses correctly if applicable.\\n15. coordinator assigns one UAV.\\n16. assigned UAV flies physically to standoff.\\n17. RViz clearly shows assignment/standoff.\\n18. operator receives alert.\\n19. operator selects RETURN or CONTINUE_HOLD.\\n20. UAV responds correctly.\\n21. remaining UAVs continue according to mission state.\\n22. returning vehicle uses PX4 RTL/landing.\\n23. vehicle lands and disarms.\\n24. docking/state accounting updates.\\n25. mission reaches correct final state.\\n\\nThe examiner must be able to understand the process visually and from status output.\\n\\n==================================================\\n35. BACKEND SEPARATION MUST REMAIN\\n==================================================\\n\\nDo not make every demonstration require every dependency.\\n\\nMaintain distinct testing modes.\\n\\nMODE A\\n\\nlogic UAV\\n+\\ndetector_sim\\n\\nMODE B\\n\\nPX4 UAV\\n+\\ndetector_sim\\n\\nMODE C\\n\\nPX4 UAV\\n+\\nGazebo camera\\n+\\nOpenCV\\n\\nMODE D\\n\\nPX4 UAV\\n+\\nGazebo camera\\n+\\noptional YOLO\\n\\nMODE E\\n\\nmulti-PX4\\n+\\ndetector_sim\\n\\nMODE F\\n\\nmulti-PX4\\n+\\nselected real camera perception\\n\\nThis modularity is important for debugging and academic demonstration reliability.\\n\\n==================================================\\n36. DEPENDENCY POLICY\\n==================================================\\n\\nKeep the currently validated V0.3 dependency pins unless there is a demonstrated incompatibility.\\n\\nDo not upgrade PX4, ROS, Gazebo, XRCE-DDS, or related tools merely because newer versions exist.\\n\\nAny dependency change must answer:\\n\\n- Why is it necessary?\\n- What current limitation does it solve?\\n- What does it risk breaking?\\n- Was V0.3 regression-tested afterward?\\n\\nPotential new V0.4 dependency:\\n\\nYOLO / Ultralytics\\n\\nOnly if used.\\n\\nDo not add:\\n\\n- micro-ROS without embedded hardware;\\n- Nav2 without explicit UGV autonomous-navigation need;\\n- another middleware;\\n- unnecessary optimization libraries.\\n\\n==================================================\\n37. HOLD was retained for three seconds before RETURN.\\n\\n## Integration issues found and fixed\\n\\n- The first run remained STORED without QGC. Added required IMU/NavSat/Magnetometer/AirPressure systems and spherical coordinates. Preflight then passed.\\n- Readiness filters active deployment IDs and expired readiness; the adapter queues unready LAUNCH and honors early mission overrides.\\n- Command ACKs for another external component cannot fault this adapter. Faulted RESUME stays inhibited even with stale telemetry. Fresh faults publish PAUSED; explicit RETURN can delegate RTL and report measured docking without restarting Offboard.\\n- Runtime owns its child process groups and ends them on exit. No unrelated process is killed. DOCKED means landed/disarmed at home, not physical tray contact. PX4 RTL uses its own altitude/speed configuration; the adapter's 60 m horizontal / 30 m vertical bounds constrain ROS Offboard position intent. ROS search_speed does not set PX4 velocity limits. The existing OpenCV detector's approximate projection is not calibrated map geolocation. 0.0\\n        self.origin = tuple(float(self.get_parameter(f'map_origin_{axis}').value) for axis in 'xyz')\\n        self.home_z = 0.0\\n        self.pending_launch = False\\n        self.ready_pub = self.create_publisher(Bool, f'/drones/{self.id}/ready', 10)\\n        self.heartbeat_pub = self.create_publisher(OffboardControlMode, self.topic('in', 'offboard_control_mode', OffboardControlMode), 10)\\n        self.setpoint_pub = self.create_publisher(TrajectorySetpoint, self.topic('in', 'trajectory_setpoint', TrajectorySetpoint), 10)\\n        self.command_pub = name, msg_type), callback, qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n        self.get_logger().info('Single-UAV PX4 backend ready; awaiting telemetry and typed LAUNCH')\\n\\n    def topic(self, direction, name, msg_type):\\n        return versioned_topic(self.prefix, direction, name, msg_type)\\n\\n    def status_cb(self, msg):\\n        self.status = msg\\n        self.received['status'] = time.monotonic()\\n\\n    def land_cb(self, msg):\\n        self.land = msg\\n        self.received['land'] = time.monotonic()\\n        if not msg.landed:\\n            self.was_airborne = True\\n\\n msg):\\n        if not (msg.xy_valid and msg.z_valid and all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (msg.x, msg.y, msg.z, msg.heading))):\\n            self.received.pop('local', None)\\n            return\\n origin reset in flight; stop external control')\\n        self.reset_counters = counters\\n        self.local = msg\\n        local = ned_to_enu((msg.x, msg.y, msg.z))\\n        self.x, self.y, self.z = tuple(local[i] + self.origin[i] for i in range(3))\\n        self.yaw = math.pi / 2 - msg.heading\\n        self.received['local'] = time.monotonic()\\n\\n    def fresh(self):\\n        timeout = float(self.get_parameter('telemetry_timeout').value)\\n        now = time.monotonic()\\n        return all(now - self.received.get(key, -math.inf) < timeout for key in ('status', 'local', 'land'))\\n\\n    def fail(self, reason):\\n        if self.fault is None:\\n            self.get_logger().error(reason)\\n        self.fault = reason\\n        self.state = DroneState.PAUSED\\n\\n    def ready(self):\\n        return (self.fresh() and self.fault is None and self.status.pre_flight_checks_pass\\n                and self.land.landed and self.state == DroneState.STORED\\n                and not self.launch_inhibited)\\n\\n    def command_cb(self, msg):\\n        if msg.drone_id != self.id:\\n            return\\n        if msg.command == DroneCommand.LAUNCH:\\n            if self.state == DroneState.STORED and not self.launch_inhibited:\\n                self.pending_launch = True\\n            return\\n        if msg.command == DroneCommand.RETURN_HOME:\\n            self.pending_launch = False\\n        if self.fault:\\n            # A fault cannot resume the ROS FSM, including while telemetry is stale.\\n            if msg.command == DroneCommand.RETURN_HOME and self.fresh():\\n                self.state = DroneState.RETURNING\\n                self.send_command(VehicleCommand.VEHICLE_CMD_NAV_RETURN_TO_LAUNCH)\\n            return\\n        # Mission overrides latch even when telemetry is not ready.\\n        if not self.fresh():\\n            super().command_cb(msg)\\n            return\\n        if msg.command == DroneCommand.HOLD_POSITION:\\n return\\n        self.pending_launch = False\\n        self.home_x, self.home_y, self.home_z = self.x, self.y, self.z\\n        self.goal = (self.home_x, self.home_y, self.home_z + self.alt)\\n        super().command_cb(DroneCommand(drone_id=self.id, command=DroneCommand.LAUNCH))\\n\\n    def ack_cb(self, msg):\\n        if (msg.target_system != 255 or msg.target_component != 1\\n                or msg.from_external = True\\n        self.command_pub.publish(msg)\\n        self.last_commands[command] = now\\n        self.command_attempts[command] = attempts + 1\\n\\n    def stream(self):\\n local_goal = tuple(self.goal[i] - self.origin[i] for i in range(3))\\n        goal.position = enu_to_ned(local_goal)\\n        goal.velocity = [math.nan] * 3\\n        goal.acceleration = [math.nan] * 3\\n        goal.jerk = [math.nan] * 3\\n        goal.yaw = math.nan\\n        goal.yawspeed = math.nan\\n        self.heartbeat_pub.publish(mode)\\n        self.setpoint_pub.publish(goal)\\n        #       "text": "{\"status\":\"fulfilled\",\"value\":{\"chunk_id\":\"2784a0\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000034185,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":3084,\"output\":\"import time\\nimport rclpy\\nfrom rclpy.node import Node\\nfrom rclpy.qos import QoSProfile, DurabilityPolicy\\n\\n# Retain one-shot deployment events for nodes joining during launch.\\nDEPLOYMENT_QOS = QoSProfile(depth=32, durability=DurabilityPolicy.TRANSIENT_LOCAL)\\nfrom std_msgs.msg import Bool\\nfrom wildlife_interfaces.msg import DroneCommand, UGVState, DeploymentRequest, TraySlot\\n\\nTRAY={1:('uav01','uav02'),2:('uav03','uav04'),3:('uav05','uav06'),4:('uav07','uav08')}\\n\\nclass DeploymentNode(Node):\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        super().__init__('deployment_manager')\\n        self.declare_parameter('inter_drone_delay',3.0); self.declare_parameter('deployment_mode','SINGLE')\\n        self.declare_parameter('active_drone_ids', [f'uav{i:02d}' for i in range(1, 9)])\\n        self.declare_parameter('require_ready', False)\\n        self.active_ids = set(self.get_parameter('active_drone_ids').value)\\n        self.require_ready = bool(self.get_parameter('require_ready').value)\\n        self.readiness = {}\\n        for did in sorted(self.active_ids):\\n            self.create_subscription(Bool, f'/drones/{did}/ready',\\n                                     lambda msg, did=did: self.readiness.update({did: (msg.data, time.monotonic())}), 10)\\n        self.delay=float(self.get_parameter('inter_drone_delay').value); self.default_mode=str(self.get_parameter('deployment_mode').value)\\n        self.cmd_pub=self.create_publisher(DroneCommand,'/deployment/commands',DEPLOYMENT_QOS); self.status_pub=self.create_publisher(TraySlot,'/deployment/status',20)\\n        self.create_subscription(DeploymentRequest,'/ugv/deployment_request',self.request_cb,DEPLOYMENT_QOS)\\n        self.blocked = set()\\n        self.cancelled = set()\\n        self.create_subscription(DroneCommand, '/mission/drone_commands', self.control_cb, 20)\\n        self.sent=set(); self.pending=[]; self.last_send=0.0; self.active_slots=set(); self.create_timer(0.1,self.tick)\\n    def control_cb(self, msg):\\n        if msg.command == DroneCommand.HOLD_POSITION:\\n            self.blocked.add(msg.drone_id)\\n        elif msg.command == DroneCommand.RESUME:\\n            self.blocked.discard(msg.drone_id)\\n        elif msg.command == DroneCommand.RETURN_HOME:\\n            self.cancelled.add(msg.drone_id)\\n            self.pending = [item for item in self.pending if item[0] != msg.drone_id]\\n\\n    def request_cb(self,msg):\\n        mode=msg.deployment_mode or self.default_mode; wp=int(msg.waypoint_index)\\n        if mode=='SINGLE' and wp==0: slots=[1,2,3,4]\\n        elif mode=='SPLIT' and wp==0: slots=[1,2]\\n        elif mode=='SPLIT' and wp==1: slots=[3,4]\\n        else: slots=[]\\n        for slot in slots:\\n            u,l=TRAY[slot]\\n            for did,upper in ((u,True),(l,False)):\\n                if did in self.active_ids and did not in self.cancelled and did not in self.sent and not any(item[0] == did for item in self.pending): self.pending.append((did,slot,upper))\\n        self.get_logger().info(f'Deployment request WP{wp} mode={mode}: {slots}')\\n    def tick(self):\\n        if not self.pending or time.monotonic()-self.last_send < self.delay: return\\n        did = self.pending[0][0]\\n        ready, received = self.readiness.get(did, (False, 0.0))\\n        if self.require_ready and (not ready or time.monotonic() - received > 1.0):\\n            return\\n        if self.pending[0][0] in self.blocked:\\n            return\\n        did,slot,upper=self.pending.pop(0)\\n        if did in self.sent: return\\n        cmd=DroneCommand(); cmd.drone_id=did; cmd.command=DroneCommand.LAUNCH; self.cmd_pub.publish(cmd); self.sent.add(did); self.active_slots.add(slot); self.last_send=time.monotonic()\\n        self.publish_slot(slot); self.get_logger().info(f'Launch command: {did} slot={slot} {\\\"UPPER\\\" if upper else \\\"LOWER\\\"}')\\n    def publish_slot(self,slot):\\n        u,l=TRAY[slot]; m=TraySlot(); m.slot_id=slot; m.upper_drone=u; m.lower_drone=l; m.upper_launched=u in self.sent; m.lower_launched=l in self.sent; self.status_pub.publish(m)\\n\\ndef main(args=None):\\n    rclpy.init(args=args); n=DeploymentNode(); rclpy.spin(n); n.destroy_node(); rclpy.shutdown()\\nimport math, random, time\\nimport rclpy\\nfrom rclpy.node import Node\\nfrom wildlife_interfaces.msg import Detection, DroneState\\nclass DetectorSim(Node):\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        super().__init__('perception_detector')\\n        for k,v in {'drone_id':'uav01','random_seed':0,'detection_range':18.0,'detection_probability':0.55,'confidence_noise_sigma':0.03,'position_noise_sigma':0.25,'confidence_threshold':0.50,'publish_period':1.0,'target_positions':[15.,12.,25.,8.],'target_classes':['dummy_animal','dummy_animal']}.items(): self.declare_parameter(k,v)\\n        g=lambda k:self.get_parameter(k).value; self.id=str(g('drone_id')); self.range=float(g('detection_range')); self.prob=float(g('detection_probability')); self.csig=float(g('confidence_noise_sigma')); self.psig=float(g('position_noise_sigma')); self.thr=float(g('confidence_threshold')); self.x=self.y=self.z=0.; self.state=DroneState.STORED\\n        self.rng = random.Random(f\\\"{g('random_seed')}:{self.id}\\\")\\n        self.sequence = 0\\n        flat=list(g('target_positions')); classes=list(g('target_classes')); self.targets=[(f'dummy_{i+1:03d}',classes[i] if i<len(classes) else 'dummy_animal',float(x),float(y)) for i,(x,y) in enumerate(zip(flat[::2],flat[1::2]))]\\n        self.pub=self.create_publisher(Detection,f'/drones/{self.id}/detections',20); self.create_subscription(DroneState,'/drones/state',self.state_cb,50); self.create_timer(float(g('publish_period')),self.tick)\\n    def state_cb(self,m):\\n        if m.drone_id==self.id: self.x=m.pose.pose.position.x; self.y=m.pose.pose.position.y; self.z=m.pose.pose.position.z; self.state=m.state\\n    def tick(self):\\n        if self.state not in (DroneState.SEARCHING,DroneState.STANDOFF): return\\n        for tid,cls,tx,ty in self.targets:\\n            d=math.hypot(tx-self.x,ty-self.y)\\n            if d>self.range or self.rng.random()>self.prob: continue\\n            conf=max(0.,min(1.,1.-d/self.range+self.rng.gauss(0,self.csig)))\\n            if conf<self.thr: continue\\n            self.sequence += 1\\n            m=Detection(); m.header.stamp=self.get_clock().now().to_msg(); m.drone_id=self.id; m.detection_id=f'{self.id}_{tid}_{self.sequence:06d}'; m.target_class=cls; m.confidence=conf; m.position_world.x=tx+self.rng.gauss(0,self.psig); m.position_world.y=ty+self.rng.gauss(0,self.psig); m.position_world.z=0.; m.covariance_xx=self.psig**2; m.covariance_yy=self.psig**2; m.distance_from_drone=d; self.pub.publish(m)\\ndef main(args=None):\\n    rclpy.init(args=args); n=DetectorSim(); rclpy.spin(n); n.destroy_node(); rclpy.shutdown()\\nimport time\\nimport rclpy\\nfrom rclpy.node import Node\\nfrom wildlife_interfaces.msg import DroneState, UGVState, Target, OperatorAlert, DroneCommand\\nfrom wildlife_interfaces.srv import MissionControl\\nclass Phase:\\n    INIT='INIT'; DEPLOYING='DEPLOYING'; SEARCHING='SEARCHING'; TARGET_FOUND='TARGET_FOUND'; AWAITING_OPERATOR='AWAITING_OPERATOR'; RESOLVING='RESOLVING'; COMPLETE='COMPLETE'; PAUSED='PAUSED'; ABORTED='ABORTED'\\nclass MissionManager(Node):\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        super().__init__('mission_manager'); self.declare_parameter('status_period',1.0); self.declare_parameter('expected_drones',8); self.phase=Phase.INIT; self.resume_phase=Phase.INIT; self.mission_id=f'M_{int(time.time())}'; self.drones={}; self.targets={}\\n        self.status_pub=self.create_publisher(__import__('std_msgs.msg',fromlist=['String']).String,'/mission/status',10); self.cmd_pub=self.create_publisher(DroneCommand,'/mission/drone_commands',20)\\n        self.create_service(MissionControl,'/mission/control',self.control_cb); self.create_subscription(UGVState,'/ugv/state',self.ugv_cb,20); self.create_subscription(DroneState,'/drones/state',self.drone_cb,50); self.create_subscription(Target,'/targets/assigned',self.target_cb,20); self.create_subscription(OperatorAlert,'/operator/alerts',self.alert_cb,20); self.create_subscription(Target,'/targets/operator_decision',self.decision_cb,20); self.create_timer(float(self.get_parameter('status_period').value),self.status)\\n    def control_cb(self,req,res):\\n        if req.command==MissionControl.Request.START:\\n            if self.phase in (Phase.ABORTED, Phase.COMPLETE):\\n                res.accepted = False\\n                res.message = 'Restart the launch for a new mission'\\n                res.mission_id = self.mission_id\\n                return res\\n            self.phase = self.resume_phase if self.phase == Phase.PAUSED else Phase.DEPLOYING\\n            self.broadcast(DroneCommand.RESUME)\\n        elif req.command == MissionControl.Request.PAUSE:\\n            if self.phase not in (Phase.PAUSED, Phase.ABORTED, Phase.COMPLETE):\\n                self.resume_phase = self.phase\\n                self.phase = Phase.PAUSED\\n                self.broadcast(DroneCommand.HOLD_POSITION)\\n        elif req.command==MissionControl.Request.ABORT: self.phase=Phase.ABORTED; self.broadcast(DroneCommand.RETURN_HOME)\\n        elif req.command == MissionControl.Request.RESET:\\n            # No cross-node reset protocol exists; never claim to reset vehicles.\\n            res.accepted = False\\n            res.mission_id = self.mission_id\\n            res.message = 'Restart the launch to reset vehicle, deployment and target state'\\n            return res\\n        else: res.accepted=False; res.mission_id=self.mission_id; res.message='Unknown command'; return res\\n        res.accepted=True; res.mission_id=self.mission_id; res.message=f'Command {req.command} applied'; return res\\n    def broadcast(self,cmd):\\n        drone_ids = set(self.drones) | {f'uav{i:02d}' for i in range(1, int(self.get_parameter('expected_drones').value) + 1)}\\n        for did in sorted(drone_ids):\\n            m=DroneCommand(); m.drone_id=did; m.command=cmd; self.cmd_pub.publish(m)\\n    def ugv_cb(self,m):\\n        if m.state==UGVState.DEPLOYING and self.phase==Phase.INIT: self.phase=Phase.DEPLOYING\\n    def drone_cb(self,m):\\n        self.drones[m.drone_id]=m\\n        if self.phase == Phase.INIT and any(d.state not in (DroneState.STORED, DroneState.DOCKED) for d in self.drones.values()):\\n            self.phase = Phase.DEPLOYING\\n        if self.phase==Phase.DEPLOYING and any(d.state==DroneState.SEARCHING for d in self.drones.values()): self.phase=Phase.SEARCHING\\n        if self.phase==Phase.ABORTED and len(self.drones) >= int(self.get_parameter(\\\"expected_drones\\\").value) and all(d.state in (DroneState.DOCKED,DroneState.STORED) for d in self.drones.values()): self.phase=Phase.COMPLETE\\n        expected = int(self.get_parameter('expected_drones').value)\\n        if self.phase not in (Phase.INIT, Phase.PAUSED, Phase.ABORTED, Phase.COMPLETE):\\n            if len(self.drones) >= expected and all(d.state == DroneState.DOCKED for d in self.drones.values()):\\n                self.phase = Phase.COMPLETE\\n            elif self.phase == Phase.AWAITING_OPERATOR and not any(d.state == DroneState.AWAITING_OPERATOR for d in self.drones.values()):\\n                self.phase = Phase.RESOLVING\\n    def target_cb(self,m): self.targets[m.target_id]=m; self.phase=Phase.TARGET_FOUND if self.phase==Phase.SEARCHING else self.phase\\n    def alert_cb(self,m):\\n        if m.severity==OperatorAlert.ACTION_REQUIRED and self.phase not in (Phase.PAUSED, Phase.ABORTED, Phase.COMPLETE): self.phase=Phase.AWAITING_OPERATOR\\n    def decision_cb(self,m):\\n        if m.target_id in self.targets: self.targets[m.target_id].status=m.status\\n        if not any(d.state==DroneState.AWAITING_OPERATOR for d in self.drones.values()) and self.phase==Phase.AWAITING_OPERATOR: self.phase=Phase.RESOLVING\\n    def status(self):\\n        s=sum(d.state==DroneState.SEARCHING for d in self.drones.values()); w=sum(d.state==DroneState.AWAITING_OPERATOR for d in self.drones.values()); r=sum(d.state==DroneState.RETURNING for d in self.drones.values()); k=sum(d.state==DroneState.DOCKED for d in self.drones.values()); line=f'[{self.mission_id}] Phase={self.phase} Targets={len(self.targets)} Searching={s} Awaiting={w} Returning={r} Docked={k}/{int(self.get_parameter(\\\"expected_drones\\\").value)}'; self.get_logger().info(line); then\\n\\n\\t\\techo \\\"INFO  [init] Setting camera to follow ${MODEL_NAME_INSTANCE}\\\"\\n\\n\\t\\t# Set camera to follow the model\\n\\t\\t${gz_command} service -s \\\"/gui/follow\\\" --reqtype gz.msgs.StringMsg \\\\\\n\\t\\t\\t\\t--reptype gz.msgs.Boolean --timeout 5000 \\\\\\n\\t\\t\\t\\t--req \\\"data: \\\\\\\"${MODEL_NAME_INSTANCE}\\\\\\\"\\\" > /dev/null 2>&1\\n\\n\\t\\t# Set default camera offset if not specified\\n\\t\\tfollow_x=${PX4_GZ_FOLLOW_OFFSET_X:--2.0}\\n\\t\\tfollow_y=${PX4_GZ_FOLLOW_OFFSET_Y:--2.0}\\n\\t\\tfollow_z=${PX4_GZ_FOLLOW_OFFSET_Z:-2.0}\\n\\n\\t\\t# Set camera offset\\n\\t\\t${gz_command} service -s \\\"/gui/follow/offset\\\" --reqtype gz.msgs.Vector3d \\\\\\n\\t\\t\\t\\t--reptype gz.msgs.Boolean --timeout 5000 \\\\\\n\\t\\t\\t\\t--req \\\"x: ${follow_x}, y: ${follow_y}, z: ${follow_z}\\\" > /dev/null 2>&1\\n\\n\\t\\techo \\\"INFO  [init] Camera follow offset set to ${follow_x}, ${follow_y}, ${follow_z}\\\"\\n\\tfi\\n\\nelif [ -n \\\"${PX4_GZ_MODEL_NAME}\\\" ]; then\\n\\t# Connect to existing model\\n\\techo \\\"INFO  [init] PX4_GZ_MODEL_NAME set, PX4 will attach to existing model\\\"\\n\\tif ! gz_bridge start -w \\\"${PX4_GZ_WORLD}\\\" -n \\\"${PX4_GZ_MODEL_NAME}\\\"; then\\n\\t\\techo \\\"ERROR [init] gz_bridge failed to start and attach to existing model\\\"\\n\\t\\texit 1\\n\\tfi\\nelse\\n\\techo \\\"ERROR [init] failed to pass either PX4_GZ_MODEL_NAME or PX4_SIM_MODEL\\\"\\n\\texit 1\\nfi\\n\\n# NOTE: Only for rover_mecanum and spacecraft_2d. All other models have\\n# the magnetometer sensor in the model.sdf.\\nif param compare -s SENS_EN_MAGSIM 1\\nthen\\n\\tsensor_mag_sim start\\nfi\\n# NOTE: new gz has airspeed sensor, remove once added\\nif param compare -s SENS_EN_ARSPDSIM 1\\nthen\\n\\tsensor_airspeed_sim start\\nfi\\n\\tparam reset_all SYS_AUTOSTART RC* CAL_* COM_FLTMODE* LND_FLIGHT* TC_* COM_FLIGHT*\\n\\tset AUTOCNF yes\\nfi\\n\\n# Allow overriding parameters via env variables: export PX4_PARAM_{name}={value}\\nenv | while IFS='=' read -r line; do\\n  value=${line#*=}\\n  name=${line%%=*}\\n  case $name in\\n    \\\"PX4_PARAM_\\\"*) param set \\\"${name#PX4_PARAM_}\\\" \\\"$value\\\" ;;\\n  esac\\ndone\\n\\n# multi-instance setup\\n# shellcheck disable=SC2154\\nparam set MAV_SYS_ID $((px4_instance+1))\\nparam set UXRCE_DDS_KEY $((px4_instance+1))\\n\\nif [ $AUTOCNF = yes ]\\nthen\\n\\tparam set SYS_AUTOSTART $SYS_AUTOSTART\\n\\n\\tparam set CAL_ACC0_ID  1310988 # 1310988: DRV_IMU_DEVTYPE_SIM, BUS: 1, ADDR: 1, TYPE: SIMULATION\\n\\tparam set CAL_GYRO0_ID 1310988 # 1310988: DRV_IMU_DEVTYPE_SIM, BUS: 1, ADDR: 1, TYPE: SIMULATION\\n\\tparam set CAL_ACC1_ID  1310996 # 1310996: DRV_IMU_DEVTYPE_SIM, BUS: 2, ADDR: 1, TYPE: SIMULATION\\n\\tparam set CAL_GYRO1_ID 1310996 # 1310996: DRV_IMU_DEVTYPE_SIM, BUS: 2, ADDR: 1, TYPE: SIMULATION\\n\\tparam set CAL_ACC2_ID  1311004 # 1311004: DRV_IMU_DEVTYPE_SIM, BUS: 3, ADDR: 1, TYPE: SIMULATION\\n\\tparam set CAL_GYRO2_ID 1311004 # 1311004: DRV_IMU_DEVTYPE_SIM, BUS: 3, ADDR: 1, TYPE: SIMULATION\\n\\n\\tparam set CAL_MAG0_ID 197388\\n\\tparam set CAL_MAG0_PRIO 50\\n\\n#\\n# Configure vehicle type specific parameters.\\n# Note: rc.vehicle_setup is the entry point for all vehicle type specific setup.\\n. ${R}etc/init.d/rc.vehicle_setup\\n\\nnavigator start\\n\\n# Try to start the UXRCE-DDS Client with UDP transport if module exists\\nuxrce_dds_ns=\\\"\\\"\\nif [ \\\"$px4_instance\\\" -ne \\\"0\\\" ]\\nthen\\n\\t# for multi intances setup, add namespace prefix\\n\\tuxrce_dds_ns=\\\"-n px4_$px4_instance\\\"\\nfi\\nif [ \\\"${PX4_UXRCE_DDS_NS+x}\\\" ]; then\\n\\t# Override, as variable is set (empty or not)\\n\\tif [ -n \\\"$PX4_UXRCE_DDS_NS\\\" ]; then\\n\\t\\t# Override namespace if environment variable is non-empty\\n\\t\\tuxrce_dds_ns=\\\"-n $PX4_UXRCE_DDS_NS\\\"\\n\\telse\\n\\t\\t# Clear namespace if variable is empty\\n\\t\\tuxrce_dds_ns=\\\"\\\"\\n\\tfi\\nfi\\nif [ -n \\\"$ROS_DOMAIN_ID\\\" ]\\nthen\\n\\t# Match UXRCE_DDS_DOM_ID with ROS_DOMAIN_ID, if defined\\n\\tparam set UXRCE_DDS_DOM_ID $ROS_DOMAIN_ID\\nelse\\n\\tparam set UXRCE_DDS_DOM_ID 0\\nfi\\nuxrce_dds_port=8888\\nif [ -n \\\"$PX4_UXRCE_DDS_PORT\\\" ]\\nthen\\n\\t# Override port if environment variable is defined\\n\\tuxrce_dds_port=\\\"$PX4_UXRCE_DDS_PORT\\\"\\nfi\\n\\nuxrce_dds_client start -t udp -p $uxrce_dds_port $uxrce_dds_ns\\n\\nif param greater -s ZENOH_ENABLE 0\\nthen\\n\\tzenoh start\\nfi\\n\\nif param greater -s MNT_MODE_IN -1\\nthen\\n\\tgimbal start\\nfi\\n\\n\"}}"

AdapterTests(unittest.TestCase):\\n    @classmethod\\n    def setUpClass(cls): rclpy.init(args=['--ros-args','-p','sitl_enabled:=true'])\\n    @classmethod\\n    def tearDownClass(cls): setattr(self.n,attr,Capture())\\n        self.n.status_cb(VehicleStatus(pre_flight_checks_pass=True, arming_state=VehicleStatus.ARMING_STATE_DISARMED))\\n        self.n.land_cb(VehicleLandDetected(landed=True))\\n        self.n.local_cb(VehicleLocalPosition(xy_valid=True,z_valid=True,x=2.,y=3.,z=-4.,heading=0.))\\n    def tearDown(self): self.n.destroy_node()\\n    def test_frame_conversion_and_versioned_topics(self):\\n        self.assertEqual(enu_to_ned((1,2,3)),[2.,1.,-3.])\\n        self.assertEqual(ned_to_enu(enu_to_ned((1,2,3))),(1.,2.,3.))\\n        self.assertEqual(versioned_topic('', 'out', 'vehicle_status',VehicleStatus),'/fmu/out/vehicle_status_v1')\\n    def test_telemetry_is_only_position_authority(self):\\n        n=self.n\\n        n.move_toward(5.,6.,10.)\\n        self.assertEqual((n.x,n.y,n.z),(3.,2.,4.))\\n        n.publish()\\n        p=n.state_pub.messages[-1].pose.pose.position\\n        self.assertEqual((p.x,p.y,p.z),(3.,2.,4.))\\n        self.assertEqual(n.state_pub.messages[-1].header.frame_id,'map')\\n    def test_invalid_position_rejects_commands(self):\\n        n=self.n\\n        n.local_cb(VehicleLocalPosition(xy_valid=False,z_valid=True))\\n        n.command_cb(DroneCommand(drone_id=n.id,command=DroneCommand.LAUNCH))\\n        self.assertEqual(n.state,DroneState.STORED)\\n        n.tick()\\n        self.assertFalse(n.heartbeat_pub.messages)\\n    def test_prestream_before_offboard_or_arm(self):\\n        n=self.n\\n        n.command_cb(DroneCommand(drone_id=n.id,command=DroneCommand.LAUNCH))\\n        n.tick()\\n        self.assertEqual(n.state,DroneState.TAKEOFF)\\n        self.assertTrue(n.heartbeat_pub.messages)\\n        self.assertFalse(n.command_pub.messages)\\n        n.stream_start=time.monotonic()-2\\n        n.tick()\\n        self.assertEqual(n.command_pub.messages[-1].command,VehicleCommand.VEHICLE_CMD_DO_SET_MODE)\\n    def test_stale_telemetry_stops_heartbeat(self):\\n        n=self.n\\n        n.stream_start=time.monotonic()-2\\n        n.received['local']=time.monotonic()-10\\n        n.tick()\\n        self.assertIsNotNone(n.fault)\\n        self.assertFalse(n.heartbeat_pub.messages)\\n    def test_origin_reset_faults_after_takeoff(self):\\n        n=self.n;n.was_airborne=True\\n        n.local_cb(VehicleLocalPosition(xy_valid=True,z_valid=True,xy_reset_counter=1))\\n        self.assertIsNotNone(n.fault)\\n    def test_rejected_command_and_bounds_fault(self):\\n        n=self.n\\n        n.send_command(VehicleCommand.VEHICLE_CMD_DO_SET_MODE,1.,6.)\\n        n.ack_cb(VehicleCommandAck(command=VehicleCommand.VEHICLE_CMD_DO_SET_MODE,result=VehicleCommandAck.VEHICLE_CMD_RESULT_DENIED,target_system=255,target_component=1))\\n        self.assertIsNotNone(n.fault)\\n        self.assertFalse(n.move_toward(1000.,0.,10.))\\n    def test_return_uses_px4_rtl_and_measured_landing(self):\\n        n=self.n;n.state=DroneState.RETURNING;n.was_airborne=True\\n        n.land_cb(VehicleLandDetected(landed=False))\\n        n.status_cb(VehicleStatus(arming_state=VehicleStatus.ARMING_STATE_ARMED))\\n        n.tick()\\n        self.assertEqual(n.command_pub.messages[-1].command,VehicleCommand.VEHICLE_CMD_NAV_RETURN_TO_LAUNCH)\\n        self.assertNotEqual(n.state,DroneState.DOCKED)\\n        n.land_cb(VehicleLandDetected(landed=True))\\n        n.status_cb(VehicleStatus(arming_state=VehicleStatus.ARMING_STATE_DISARMED))\\n        n.tick()\\n        self.assertEqual(n.state,DroneState.DOCKED)\\n\\n    def test_launch_is_queued_until_telemetry_recovers(self):\\n        n=self.n\\n        n.received.clear()\\n        n.command_cb(DroneCommand(drone_id=n.id,command=DroneCommand.LAUNCH))\\n        self.assertTrue(n.pending_launch)\\n        n.tick()\\n        self.assertEqual(n.state,DroneState.STORED)\\n        n.status_cb(VehicleStatus(pre_flight_checks_pass=True))\\n        n.land_cb(VehicleLandDetected(landed=True))\\n        n.local_cb(VehicleLocalPosition(xy_valid=True,z_valid=True))\\n        n.tick()\\n        self.assertFalse(n.pending_launch)\\n        self.assertEqual(n.state,DroneState.TAKEOFF)\\n    def test_abort_before_readiness_cancels_launch(self):\\n        n=self.n;n.received.clear()\\n        n.command_cb(DroneCommand(drone_id=n.id,command=DroneCommand.LAUNCH))\\n        n.command_cb(DroneCommand(drone_id=n.id,command=DroneCommand.RETURN_HOME))\\n        self.assertFalse(n.pending_launch)\\n        self.assertTrue(n.launch_inhibited)\\n        n.tick()\\n        self.assertFalse(n.command_pub.messages)\\n    def test_pause_before_readiness_latches_until_resume(self):\\n        n=self.n;n.received.clear()\\n        n.command_cb(DroneCommand(drone_id=n.id,command=DroneCommand.HOLD_POSITION))\\n        n.command_cb(DroneCommand(drone_id=n.id,command=DroneCommand.LAUNCH))\\n        self.assertFalse(n.pending_launch)\\n        self.assertTrue(n.launch_inhibited)\\n        n.command_cb(DroneCommand(drone_id=n.id,command=DroneCommand.RESUME))\\n        self.assertFalse(n.launch_inhibited)\\n    def test_spawn_origin_applies_in_both_directions(self):\\n        n=self.n;n.origin=(10.,20.,1.)\\n        n.local_cb(VehicleLocalPosition(xy_valid=True,z_valid=True,x=2.,y=3.,z=-4.))\\n        self.assertEqual((n.x,n.y,n.z),(13.,22.,5.))\\n        n.goal=(13.,22.,15.)\\n        n.stream()\\n        self.assertEqual(list(n.setpoint_pub.messages[-1].position),[2.,3.,-14.])\\n    def test_search_lanes_stay_bounded(self):\\n        n=self.n;n.home_z=0.\\n        n.half_width=3.;n.lane=5.\\n        visited=[]\\n        for _ in range(25):\\n            n.lawnmower_step()\\n            visited.append(n.search_goal)\\n            n.x,n.y,n.z=n.search_goal\\n        self.assertLessEqual(max(y for _,y,_ in visited),30.)\\n        self.assertGreater(len(set(visited)),2)\\n\\n    def test_other_command_sources_do_not_fault_adapter(self):\\n        n=self.n\\n        n.send_command(VehicleCommand.VEHICLE_CMD_DO_SET_MODE)\\n        n.ack_cb(VehicleCommandAck(command=VehicleCommand.VEHICLE_CMD_DO_SET_MODE,result=VehicleCommandAck.VEHICLE_CMD_RESULT_DENIED,target_system=254,target_component=190))\\n        self.assertIsNone(n.fault)\\n    def test_fault_cannot_resume_when_telemetry_is_stale(self):\\n        n=self.n;n.fail('test fault');n.received.clear()\\n        n.command_cb(DroneCommand(drone_id=n.id,command=DroneCommand.RESUME))\\n        self.assertEqual(n.state,DroneState.PAUSED)\\n        self.assertFalse(n.heartbeat_pub.messages)\\n    def test_fresh_fault_publishes_paused_and_explicit_return_can_dock(self):\\n        n=self.n;n.was_airborne=True;n.fail('test fault')\\n        n.tick()\\n self.assertFalse(n.heartbeat_pub.messages)\\n\\nif __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()\\nimport math, time\\nimport rclpy\\nfrom rclpy.node import Node\\nfrom wildlife_interfaces.msg import Detection, Target\\nclass Track:\\n    def __init__(self,det,tid,alpha):\\n        self.tid=tid; self.cls=det.target_class; self.x=det.position_world.x; self.y=det.position_world.y; self.conf=det.confidence; self.covx=det.covariance_xx; self.covy=det.covariance_yy; self.witnesses={det.drone_id}; self.count=1; self.last=time.monotonic(); self.alpha=alpha\\n    def update(self,det):\\n        self.x=(1-self.alpha)*self.x+self.alpha*det.position_world.x; self.y=(1-self.alpha)*self.y+self.alpha*det.position_world.y; self.conf=max(self.conf,det.confidence); self.covx=max(.01,self.covx*.95); self.covy=max(.01,self.covy*.95); self.witnesses.add(det.drone_id); self.count+=1; self.last=time.monotonic()\\nclass Tracker(Node):\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        super().__init__('target_tracker'); self.declare_parameter('gate_radius',3.0); self.declare_parameter('alpha',.25); self.declare_parameter('stale_timeout',15.0); self.declare_parameter('minimum_confidence',.5); self.declare_parameter('report_period',5.0)\\n        self.gate=float(self.get_parameter('gate_radius').value); self.alpha=float(self.get_parameter('alpha').value); self.timeout=float(self.get_parameter('stale_timeout').value); self.min_conf=float(self.get_parameter('minimum_confidence').value); self.tracks={}; self.next_id=1; self.pub=self.create_publisher(Target,'/targets/tracked',20); self.create_subscription(Detection,'/detections/all',self.cb,50); self.create_timer(float(self.get_parameter('report_period').value),self.expire)\\n    def cb(self,d):\\n        if d.confidence<self.min_conf: return\\n        t=self.associate(d)\\n        if t is None: t=Track(d,f'tgt_{self.next_id:03d}',self.alpha); self.next_id+=1; self.tracks[t.tid]=t\\n        else: t.update(d)\\n        self.publish(t,d.header.stamp)\\n    def associate(self,d):\\n        best=None; bd=self.gate\\n        for t in self.tracks.values():\\n            dist=math.hypot(t.x-d.position_world.x,t.y-d.position_world.y)\\n            if dist<bd and t.cls==d.target_class: best,bd=t,dist\\n        return best\\n    def publish(self,t,stamp):\\n        m=Target(); m.header.stamp=stamp; m.target_id=t.tid; m.target_class=t.cls; m.confidence=t.conf; m.pose.pose.position.x=t.x; m.pose.pose.position.y=t.y; m.detecting_drones=list(sorted(t.witnesses)); m.status=Target.UNASSIGNED; m.detection_count=t.count; m.fused_covariance_xx=t.covx; m.fused_covariance_yy=t.covy; self.pub.publish(m)\\n    def expire(self):\\n        now=time.monotonic()\\n        for t in list(self.tracks.values()):\\n            if now-t.last>self.timeout:\\n                m=Target(); m.header.stamp=self.get_clock().now().to_msg(); m.target_id=t.tid; m.target_class=t.cls; m.confidence=t.conf; m.pose.pose.position.x=t.x; m.pose.pose.position.y=t.y; m.detecting_drones=list(sorted(t.witnesses)); m.status=Target.LOST; m.detection_count=t.count; m.fused_covariance_xx=t.covx; m.fused_covariance_yy=t.covy; self.pub.publish(m); del wildlife_interfaces.msg import Target, DroneState\\nclass Coordinator(Node):\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        super().__init__('swarm_coordinator'); self.declare_parameter('assignment_period',1.0); self.declare_parameter('max_assignment_distance',50.0); self.declare_parameter('distance_weight',1.0); self.declare_parameter('target_confidence_weight',0.0)\\n        self.drones={}; self.assigned=set(); self.assigned_targets=set(); self.pub=self.create_publisher(Target,'/targets/assigned',20); self.create_subscription(Target,'/targets/unassigned',self.target_cb,20); self.create_subscription(DroneState,'/drones/state',self.drone_cb,50); self.create_timer(float(self.get_parameter('assignment_period').value),self.report)\\n    def drone_cb(self,m):\\n        self.drones[m.drone_id]=m\\n        if m.state in (DroneState.RETURNING,DroneState.DOCKING,DroneState.DOCKED,DroneState.PAUSED): self.assigned.discard(m.drone_id)\\n    def target_cb(self,t):\\n        if t.status!=Target.UNASSIGNED or t.assigned_drone_id or t.target_id in self.assigned_targets: return\\n        cands=[d for d in self.drones.values() if d.state==DroneState.SEARCHING and d.drone_id not in self.assigned]\\n        if not cands: return\\n        dw=float(self.get_parameter('distance_weight').value); cw=float(self.get_parameter('target_confidence_weight').value); maxd=float(self.get_parameter('max_assignment_distance').value)\\n        def cost(d):\\n            dist=math.hypot(d.pose.pose.position.x-t.pose.pose.position.x,d.pose.pose.position.y-t.pose.pose.position.y); return dw*dist+cw*(1.-t.confidence)\\n        best=min(cands,key=cost); dist=math.hypot(best.pose.pose.position.x-t.pose.pose.position.x,best.pose.pose.position.y-t.pose.pose.position.y)\\n        if dist>maxd: return\\n        t.assigned_drone_id=best.drone_id; t.status=Target.ASSIGNED; self.assigned.add(best.drone_id); self.assigned_targets.add(t.target_id); self.pub.publish(t); self.get_logger().info(f'Assigned {t.target_id} -> {best.drone_id} cost={cost(best):.2f}')\\n    def report(self): self.get_logger().debug(f'Coordinator: {sum(d.state==DroneState.SEARCHING for d in self.drones.values())} searching | {len(self.assigned)} assigned')\\ndef main(args=None):\\n    rclpy.init(args=args); n=Coordinator(); rclpy.spin(n); n.destroy_node(); rclpy.shutdown()\\n\"}}"

self.slot_id=int(g('slot_id')); self.is_upper=bool(g('is_upper_position')); self.alt=float(g('search_altitude')); self.standoff=float(g('standoff_distance')); self.speed=float(g('search_speed')); self.loiter=float(g('loiter_duration')); self.timeout=float(g('search_timeout')); self.drain=float(g('battery_drain_per_second')); self.lane=float(g('lane_spacing')); self.half_width=float(g('search_half_width')); self.alt_offset=float(g('standoff_altitude_offset')); self.tol=float(g('motion_tolerance')); self.home_x=float(g('home_x')); self.home_y=float(g('home_y'))\\n        self.launch_inhibited = False\\n        self.resume_state = DroneState.STORED\\n        self.pause_started = None\\n        self.state=DroneState.STORED; self.x=self.y=self.z=0.; self.battery=100.; self.target=None; self.search_start=None; self.loiter_start=None; self.last=time.monotonic(); self.lane_dir=1.; self.hold_position=False; self.alerted_target=None\\n        self.state_pub=self.create_publisher(DroneState,'/drones/state',20); self.alert_pub=self.create_publisher(OperatorAlert,'/operator/alerts',20)\\n        self.create_subscription(DroneCommand,'/deployment/commands',self.command_cb,DEPLOYMENT_QOS); self.create_subscription(DroneCommand,'/mission/drone_commands',self.command_cb,20); self.create_subscription(Target,'/targets/assigned',self.target_cb,20); self.create_subscription(Target,'/targets/operator_decision',self.decision_cb,20)\\n        self.create_timer(0.1,self.tick)\\n    def command_cb(self,m):\\n        if m.drone_id!=self.id: return\\n        if m.command in (DroneCommand.HOLD_POSITION, DroneCommand.RETURN_HOME):\\n            self.launch_inhibited = True\\n        elif m.command == DroneCommand.RESUME:\\n            self.launch_inhibited = False\\n        if m.command==DroneCommand.LAUNCH and self.state==DroneState.STORED and not self.launch_inhibited: self.state=DroneState.LAUNCHING; self.hold_position=False\\n        elif m.command==DroneCommand.RETURN_HOME and self.state not in (DroneState.STORED,DroneState.DOCKED): self.state=DroneState.RETURNING; self.hold_position=False\\n        elif m.command == DroneCommand.HOLD_POSITION and self.state not in (DroneState.STORED, DroneState.DOCKED, DroneState.PAUSED):\\n            self.resume_state = self.state\\n            self.pause_started = time.monotonic()\\n            self.state = DroneState.PAUSED\\n            self.hold_position = True\\n        elif m.command==DroneCommand.GO_TO_STANDOFF and self.target: self.state=DroneState.STANDOFF; self.hold_position=False\\n        elif m.command == DroneCommand.RESUME and self.state == DroneState.PAUSED:\\n            paused_for = time.monotonic() - self.pause_started\\n            if self.search_start is not None:\\n                self.search_start += paused_for\\n            if self.loiter_start is not None:\\n                self.loiter_start += paused_for\\n            self.state = self.resume_state\\n            self.hold_position = False\\n    def target_cb(self,m):\\n        if m.assigned_drone_id==self.id and m.target_id and self.state==DroneState.SEARCHING: self.target=m; self.state=DroneState.TARGET_DETECTED; self.alerted_target=None\\n    def decision_cb(self,m):\\n        if m.assigned_drone_id!=self.id or m.target_id!=(self.target.target_id if self.target else ''): return\\n        if self.state!=DroneState.AWAITING_OPERATOR: return\\n        if m.status==Target.CLEARED: self.state=DroneState.RETURNING\\n        elif m.status == Target.STANDOFF_HOLD:\\n            # Stay available for a later operator RETURN without another alert.\\n            self.hold_position = True\\n    def tick(self):\\n        self.drain_battery(); s=self.state\\n        if s==DroneState.LAUNCHING: self.state=DroneState.TAKEOFF\\n        elif s==DroneState.TAKEOFF:\\n            self.z=min(self.alt,self.z+self.speed*0.1)\\n            if self.z>=self.alt: self.state=DroneState.SEARCHING; self.search_start=time.monotonic()\\n        elif s==DroneState.SEARCHING:\\n            self.lawnmower_step()\\n            if self.search_start and time.monotonic()-self.search_start>=self.timeout: self.state=DroneState.LOITERING; self.loiter_start=time.monotonic()\\n        elif s==DroneState.LOITERING:\\n            if self.loiter_start and time.monotonic()-self.loiter_start>=self.loiter: self.state=DroneState.RETURNING\\n        elif s==DroneState.TARGET_DETECTED: self.state=DroneState.STANDOFF\\n        elif s==DroneState.STANDOFF and self.target:\\n            if self.move_to_standoff(): self.state=DroneState.AWAITING_OPERATOR; self.send_alert_once()\\n        elif s==DroneState.RETURNING:\\n            if self.move_toward(self.home_x,self.home_y,0.): self.state=DroneState.DOCKING\\n        elif s==DroneState.DOCKING:\\n            self.z=max(0.,self.z-self.speed*0.1)\\n            if self.z<=0.: self.state=DroneState.DOCKED\\n        self.publish()\\n    def lawnmower_step(self):\\n        self.x+=self.lane_dir*self.speed*0.1\\n        if abs(self.x)>=self.half_width: self.lane_dir*=-1.; self.y+=self.lane\\n    def move_to_standoff(self):\\n        if not self.target: return False\\n        p=self.target.pose.pose.position; return self.move_toward(p.x,p.y+self.standoff,self.alt+self.alt_offset)\\n    def move_toward(self,tx,ty,tz):\\n        dx,dy,dz=tx-self.x,ty-self.y,tz-self.z; d=math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz)\\n        if d<=self.tol: self.x,self.y,self.z=tx,ty,tz; return True\\n        step=min(self.speed*0.1,d); self.x+=step*dx/d; self.y+=step*dy/d; self.z+=step*dz/d; return False\\n    def send_alert_once(self):\\n        if not self.target or self.alerted_target==self.target.target_id: return\\n        a=OperatorAlert(); a.header.stamp=self.get_clock().now().to_msg(); a.alert_id=f'alert_{self.id}_{self.target.target_id}'; a.drone_id=self.id; a.target_id=self.target.target_id; a.target_class=self.target.target_class; a.confidence=self.target.confidence; a.target_position=self.target.pose.pose.position; a.alert_message=f'{self.id} is holding standoff on {self.target.target_class}; operator decision required.'; a.severity=OperatorAlert.ACTION_REQUIRED; self.alert_pub.publish(a); self.alerted_target=self.target.target_id\\n    def drain_battery(self):\\n        now=time.monotonic(); dt=now-self.last; self.last=now\\n        if self.state not in (DroneState.STORED,DroneState.DOCKED): self.battery=max(0.,self.battery-self.drain*dt)\\n    def publish(self):\\n        m=DroneState(); m.header.stamp=self.get_clock().now().to_msg(); m.drone_id=self.id; m.state=self.state; m.pose.pose.position.x=self.x; m.pose.pose.position.y=self.y; m.pose.pose.position.z=self.z; m.battery_percent=self.battery; m.standoff_distance=self.standoff; m.slot_id=self.slot_id; m.is_upper_position=self.is_upper; m.assigned_target_id=self.target.target_id if self.target else ''; self.state_pub.publish(m)\\n\\ndef main(args=None):\\n    rclpy.init(args=args); n=UAVNode(); rclpy.spin(n); n.destroy_node(); rclpy.shutdown()\\nimport rclpy\\nfrom rclpy.node import Node\\nfrom wildlife_interfaces.msg import Target\\nclass TargetManager(Node):\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        super().__init__('target_manager'); self.declare_parameter('report_period',5.0); self.db={}; self.registry_pub=self.create_publisher(Target,'/targets/registry',20); self.unassigned_pub=self.create_publisher(Target,'/targets/unassigned',20); self.create_subscription(Target,'/targets/tracked',self.tracked_cb,30); self.create_subscription(Target,'/targets/assigned',self.assigned_cb,20); self.create_subscription(Target,'/targets/operator_decision',self.decision_cb,20); self.create_timer(float(self.get_parameter('report_period').value),self.report)\\n    def tracked_cb(self,t):\\n        old=self.db.get(t.target_id)\\n        if old and t.status != Target.LOST:\\n            t.status=old.status; t.assigned_drone_id=old.assigned_drone_id; t.operator_notified=old.operator_notified\\n        self.db[t.target_id]=t; self.publish(t)\\n    def assigned_cb(self,t):\\n        cur=self.db.get(t.target_id,t); cur.assigned_drone_id=t.assigned_drone_id; cur.status=Target.ASSIGNED; self.db[t.target_id]=cur; self.publish(cur)\\n    def decision_cb(self,t):\\n        if t.target_id not in self.db: return\\n        cur=self.db[t.target_id]; cur.status=t.status; cur.assigned_drone_id=t.assigned_drone_id or cur.assigned_drone_id; cur.operator_notified=True; self.db[t.target_id]=cur; self.publish(cur)\\n    def publish(self,t):\\n        self.registry_pub.publish(t)\\n        if t.status==Target.UNASSIGNED: self.unassigned_pub.publish(t)\\n    def report(self):\\n        counts={}\\n        for t in self.db.values(): counts[t.status]=counts.get(t.status,0)+1\\n        self.get_logger().info(f'Target registry: {len(self.db)} targets | {counts}')\\ndef main(args=None):\\n    rclpy.init(args=args); n=TargetManager(); rclpy.spin(n); n.destroy_node(); rclpy.shutdown()\\n\"}}"

  "input": "text(await tools.apply_patch(\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Add File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/tests/px4_two_isolation_probe.py\\n+\\\"\\\"\\\"Live two-PX4 command, telemetry, ACK and map-origin isolation gate.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n+import argparse\\n+import json\\n+import math\\n+import time\\n+\\n+import rclpy\\n+from rclpy.node import Node\\n+from rclpy.qos import DurabilityPolicy, QoSProfile, qos_profile_sensor_data\\n+from px4_msgs.msg import (VehicleCommand, VehicleCommandAck, VehicleLandDetected,\\n+                          VehicleLocalPosition, VehicleStatus)\\n+from wildlife_interfaces.msg import DroneCommand, DroneState\\n+from wildlife_simulation.px4_uav_adapter import versioned_topic\\n+\\n+\\n+parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n+parser.add_argument('--output', required=True)\\n+args = parser.parse_args()\\n+ids = ('uav01', 'uav02')\\n+origins = {'uav01': (0., 0.), 'uav02': (0., -35.)}\\n+rclpy.init()\\n+node = Node('px4_two_isolation_probe')\\n+states = {}\\n+seen_states = {did: set() for did in ids}\\n+raw = {did: {} for did in ids}\\n+commands = {did: [] for did in ids}\\n+acks = {did: [] for did in ids}\\n+ready = {did: False for did in ids}\\n+flight = {did: {'offboard': False, 'armed': False, 'gcs': False,\\n+                'min_x': math.inf, 'max_x': -math.inf} for did in ids}\\n+failures = []\\n+\\n+\\n+def state_cb(msg):\\n+    if msg.drone_id not in ids:\\n+        failures.append(f'unexpected DroneState identity {msg.drone_id}')\\n+        return\\n+    did = msg.drone_id\\n+    states[did] = msg\\n+    seen_states[did].add(msg.state)\\n+    x = msg.pose.pose.position.x\\n+    flight[did]['min_x'] = min(flight[did]['min_x'], x)\\n+    flight[did]['max_x'] = max(flight[did]['max_x'], x)\\n+    local = raw[did].get('local')\\n+    if local and local.xy_valid and local.z_valid:\\n+        ox, oy = origins[did]\\n+        if math.hypot(x - (local.y + ox), msg.pose.pose.position.y - (local.x + oy)) > 3.0:\\n+            failures.append(f'{did} DroneState does not match its own PX4 local position')\\n+\\n+\\n+node.create_subscription(DroneState, '/drones/state', state_cb, 50)\\n+for did in ids:\\n+    prefix = '/' + did\\n+\\n+    def status_cb(msg, did=did):\\n+        raw[did]['status'] = msg\\n+        flight[did]['gcs'] |= not msg.gcs_connection_lost\\n+        flight[did]['offboard'] |= msg.nav_state == VehicleStatus.NAVIGATION_STATE_OFFBOARD\\n+        flight[did]['armed'] |= msg.arming_state == VehicleStatus.ARMING_STATE_ARMED\\n+\\n+    node.create_subscription(VehicleStatus,\\n+                             versioned_topic(prefix, 'out', 'vehicle_status', VehicleStatus),\\n+                             status_cb, qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n+    node.create_subscription(VehicleLocalPosition,\\n+                             versioned_topic(prefix, 'out', 'vehicle_local_position', VehicleLocalPosition),\\n+                             lambda msg, did=did: raw[did].update(local=msg), qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n+    node.create_subscription(VehicleLandDetected,\\n+                             versioned_topic(prefix, 'out', 'vehicle_land_detected', VehicleLandDetected),\\n+                             lambda msg, did=did: raw[did].update(land=msg), qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n+    node.create_subscription(VehicleCommand,\\n+                             versioned_topic(prefix, 'in', 'vehicle_command', VehicleCommand),\\n+                             lambda msg, did=did: commands[did].append(msg), qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n+    node.create_subscription(VehicleCommandAck,\\n+                             versioned_topic(prefix, 'out', 'vehicle_command_ack', VehicleCommandAck),\\n+                             lambda msg, did=did: acks[did].append(msg), qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n+    from std_msgs.msg import Bool\\n+    node.create_subscription(Bool, f'/drones/{did}/ready',\\n+                             lambda msg, did=did: ready.update({did: ready[did] or msg.data}), 10)\\n+\\n+deploy_qos = QoSProfile(depth=32, durability=DurabilityPolicy.TRANSIENT_LOCAL)\\n+deploy = node.create_publisher(DroneCommand, '/deployment/commands', deploy_qos)\\n+mission = node.create_publisher(DroneCommand, '/mission/drone_commands', 20)\\n+\\n+\\n+def wait_for(label, condition, seconds):\\n+    deadline = time.monotonic() + seconds\\n+    while time.monotonic() < deadline:\\n+        rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=.1)\\n+        if failures:\\n+            raise AssertionError(failures[0])\\n+        if condition():\\n+            print('PASS', label, flush=True)\\n+            return\\n+    raise TimeoutError(label)\\n+\\n+\\n+def send(pub, did, command):\\n+    pub.publish(DroneCommand(drone_id=did, command=command))\\n+\\n+\\n+start = time.monotonic()\\n+result = {'result': 'FAIL', 'stages': [], 'failures': failures}\\n+try:\\n+    wait_for('both vehicles ready', lambda: all(ready.values()) and\\n+             all('local' in Setting `ROS_LOG_DIR` to `/tmp` resolved this environment issue without source changes.\\n+\\n+The pinned PX4 SITL and agent binaries were present and exercised by the live missions; neither upstream dependency was rebuilt for this gate.\\n+\\n+## Gates 2–3 — two-PX4 namespace and command isolation: PASS\\n+\\n+Run: `scripts/validate_px4_two.sh` with an active QGroundControl UDP link.\\n+Result: `validation/px4_two_isolation.json`.\\n+\\n+| Vehicle | PX4 instance | MAV system ID / DDS key | DDS namespace | Gazebo entity | spawn / ROS map origin |\\n+|---|---:|---:|---|---|---|\\n+| uav01 | 0 | 1 | /uav01 | x500_0 | (0, 0) m |\\n+| uav02 | 1 | 2 | /uav02 | x500_1 | (0, -35) m |\\n+\\n+The probe saw both vehicles ready and separate PX4 telemetry publishers. Commands on each namespaced input topic targeted only system ID 1 or 2 respectively; each namespaced ACK stream contained accepted, source-correlated responses. Both returned, landed and disarmed. states[msg.drone_id] = msg\\n+        histories[msg.drone_id].add(msg.state)\\n+\\n+\\n+def assignment_cb(msg):\\n+    global second_searched_during_assignment\\n+ phases.append(phase)\\n+\\n+\\n+node.create_subscription(DroneState, '/drones/state', state_cb, 50)\\n+node.create_subscription(Target, '/targets/assigned', assignment_cb, node.create_subscription(VehicleStatus,\\n+                             versioned_topic(prefix, 'out', 'vehicle_status', VehicleStatus),\\n+                             status_cb,                      'slot_id': index // 2 + 1,\\n+                               'is_upper_position': index % 2 == 0,\\n+                               'search_altitude': 10.0 + 1.5 * index,\\n+                               'map_origin_x': x, 'map_origin_y': y,\\n+                           }]))\\n+    return result\\n+\\n+\\n+def generate_launch_description():\\n+    return 18, -18 if i < 4 else 18)\\n+           for i, did in enumerate(ids)}\\n+rclpy.init()\\n+node = Node(f'px4_{args.count}_fleet_probe')\\n+status = {}\\n+local = {}\\n+states = {}\\n+commands = {did: 0 for did in ids}\\n+ready = {did: False for did in ids}\\n+gcs = {did: False for did in ids}\\n+\\n+\\n+def state_cb(msg):\\n+    if msg.drone_id in ids:\\n+        states[msg.drone_id] = msg\\n+\\n+\\n+node.create_subscription(DroneState, '/drones/state', state_cb, 100)\\n+for did in ids:\\n+    prefix = '/' + did\\n+\\n+    def status_cb(msg, did=did):\\n+        status[did] = msg\\n+        gcs[did] |= not msg.gcs_connection_lost\\n+\\n+    node.create_subscription(VehicleStatus,\\n+                             versioned_topic(prefix, 'out', 'vehicle_status', VehicleStatus),\\n+                             status_cb, qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n+    node.create_subscription(VehicleLocalPosition,\\n+                             versioned_topic(prefix, 'out', 'vehicle_local_position', VehicleLocalPosition),\\n+                             lambda msg, did=did: local.update({did: msg}), qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n+    node.create_subscription(VehicleCommand,\\n+                             versioned_topic(prefix, 'in', 'vehicle_command', VehicleCommand),\\n+                             lambda msg, did=did: commands.update({did: commands[did] + 1}), qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n+    node.create_subscription(Bool, f'/drones/{did}/ready',\\n+                             lambda msg, did=did: ready.update({did: ready[did] or msg.data}), 10)\\n+\\n+start = time.monotonic()\\n+result = {'result': 'FAIL', 'failure': ''}\\n+try:\\n+    deadline = start + (160 if args.count == 8 else 100)\\n+    while time.monotonic() < deadline and not all(ready.values()):\\n+        rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=.1)\\n+        if int(time.monotonic() - start) % 20 == 0 and time.monotonic() - getattr(node, 'printed', -100) > 1:\\n+            print('ready', sum(ready.values()), '/', args.count, 'telemetry', len(status), flush=True)\\n+            node.printed = time.monotonic()\\n+    assert all(ready.values()), 'not all PX4 vehicles reached ready/preflight state'\\n+    assert set(status) == set(ids) and set(local) == set(ids) and set(states) == set(ids), 'missing telemetry or DroneState'\\n+    assert all(gcs.values()), 'QGC/GCS link missing for one or more PX4 vehicles'\\n+    assert all(status[did].system_id == i + 1 for i, did in enumerate(ids)), 'MAV system ID crossover'\\n+    assert all(status[did].arming_state == VehicleStatus.ARMING_STATE_DISARMED for did in ids), 'unexpected armed vehicle'\\n+    assert all(states[did].state == DroneState.STORED for did in ids), 'unexpected deployment'\\n+    assert all(count == 0 for count in commands.values()), 'PX4 command sent without deployment'\\n+    assert len(node.get_publishers_info_by_topic('/drones/state')) == args.count, 'DroneState publisher count'\\n+    for did in ids:\\n+        ox, oy = origins[did]\\n+        p = states[did].pose.pose.position\\n+        measured = local[did]\\n+        assert measured.xy_valid and measured.z_valid, f'{did} invalid PX4 position'\\n+        assert math.hypot(p.x - ox, p.y - oy) < 3., f'{did} wrong map origin'\\n+        assert math.hypot(p.x - (measured.y + ox), p.y - (measured.x + oy)) < 3., f'{did} map/local mismatch'\\n+        topic = versioned_topic('/' + did, 'out', 'vehicle_status', VehicleStatus)\\n+        assert len(node.get_publishers_info_by_topic(topic)) == 1, f'{did} telemetry publisher count'\\n+        log = Path(os.environ['WILDLIFE_RUN_DIR']) / f'{did}.log'\\n+        assert f'model: x500_{int(did[-2:]) - 1}' in log.read_text(), f'{did} Gazebo entity missing'\\n+    result['result'] = 'PASS'\\n+except AssertionError as exc:\\n+    result['failure'] = str(exc)\\n+finally:\\n+    cpu = subprocess.run(['ps', '-C', 'px4', '-o', 'pid=,%cpu=,rss='],\\n+                         capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)\\n+    result.update(count=args.count, elapsed_s=round(time.monotonic() - start, 1),\\n+                  ready=ready, gcs=gcs,\\n+                  system_ids={did: status[did].system_id if did in status else None for did in ids},\\n+                  drone_states={did: states[did].state if did in states else None for did in ids},\\n+                  local_valid={did: bool(did in local and local[did].xy_valid and local[did].z_valid) for did in ids},\\n+                  command_counts=commands,\\n+                  px4_process_sample=cpu.stdout.strip().splitlines())\\n+    with open(args.output, 'w') as stream:\\n+        json.dump(result, stream, indent=2)\\n+        stream.write('\\\\n')\\n+    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2), flush=True)\\n+    node.destroy_node()\\n+    rclpy.shutdown()\\n+raise SystemExit(0 if result['result'] == 'PASS' else 1)\\n*** Add File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/scripts/validate_px4_fleet.sh\\n+#!/usr/bin/env bash\\n+set -e\\n+if [[ \\\"$#\\\" -ne 1 || ( \\\"$1\\\" != 4 && \\\"$1\\\" != 8 ) ]]; then\\n+  echo 'Usage: scripts/validate_px4_fleet.sh 4|8' >&2\\n+  exit 2\\n+fi\\n+count=\\\"$1\\\"\\n+source \\\"$(dirname \\\"$0\\\")/environment.sh\\\"\\n+export ROS_DOMAIN_ID=85\\n+export GZ_PARTITION=wildlife-v04-fleet\\n+export ROS_LOG_DIR=\\\"/tmp/wildlife-v04-fleet-${count}-logs\\\"\\n+export WILDLIFE_RUN_DIR=\\\"/tmp/wildlife-v04-fleet-${count}\\\"\\n+cd \\\"$WILDLIFE_WORKSPACE_DIR\\\"\\n+python3 - <<'PY'\\n+import json\\n+from pathlib import Path\\n+base = Path('docs/validation')\\n+assert json.loads((base / 'px4_two_isolation.json').read_text())['result'] == 'PASS'\\n+assert json.loads((base / 'px4_two_mission.json').read_text())['result'] == 'PASS'\\n+PY\\n+python3 tests/px4_fleet_probe.py --count \\\"$count\\\" \\\\\\n+  --output \\\"docs/validation/px4_fleet_${count}.json\\\" & probe_pid=$!\\n+runtime_pid=\\n+cleanup() {\\n+  if [[ -n \\\"$runtime_pid\\\" ]]; then\\n+    kill -INT \\\"$runtime_pid\\\" 2>/dev/null || true\\n+    wait \\\"$runtime_pid\\\" 2>/dev/null || true\\n+  fi\\n+  if [[ -n \\\"$probe_pid\\\" ]]; then\\n+    kill \\\"$probe_pid\\\" 2>/dev/null || true\\n+    wait \\\"$probe_pid\\\" 2>/dev/null || true\\n+  fi\\n+}\\n+trap cleanup EXIT\\n+sleep 3\\n+HEADLESS=1 scripts/start_px4_fleet.sh \\\"$count\\\" & runtime_pid=$!\\n+while kill -0 \\\"$probe_pid\\\" 2>/dev/null; do\\n+  if ! kill -0 \\\"$runtime_pid\\\" 2>/dev/null; then\\n+    echo \\\"PX4 fleet runtime exited; inspect $WILDLIFE_RUN_DIR\\\" >&2\\n+    exit 1\\n+  fi\\n+  sleep .5\\n+done\\n+wait \\\"$probe_pid\\\"\\n+probe_pid=\\n+if [[ \\\"$count\\\" == 8 ]]; then\\n+  python3 - <<'PY'\\n+import json\\n+from pathlib import Path\\n+base = Path('docs/validation')\\n+assert json.loads((base / 'px4_fleet_8.json').read_text())['result'] == 'PASS'\\n+gate = base / 'px4_acceptance.json'\\n+status = json.loads(gate.read_text())\\n+status['eight_structural'] = 'PASS'\\n+gate.write_text(json.dumps(status, indent=2) + '\\\\n')\\n+PY\\n+fi\\n*** End Patch\"));\n",

It provides the real\\ncamera/OpenCV plumbing without pretending YOLO is already integrated.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport math\\nimport cv2\\nimport rclpy\\nfrom rclpy.node import Node\\nfrom sensor_msgs.msg import Image\\nfrom cv_bridge import CvBridge\\nfrom wildlife_interfaces.msg import Detection\\nfrom geometry_msgs.msg import Point\\n\\nclass OpenCVDetector(Node):\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        super().__init__('opencv_detector')\\n        self.declare_parameter('drone_id','uav01')\\n        self.declare_parameter('target_class','dummy_animal')\\n        self.declare_parameter('min_area',250.0)\\n        self.declare_parameter('camera_height_m',10.0)\\n        self.declare_parameter('hfov_deg',78.0)\\n        self.did=self.get_parameter('drone_id').value\\n        self.cls=self.get_parameter('target_class').value\\n        self.bridge=CvBridge(); area=cv2.contourArea(c)\\n        if area<float(self.get_parameter('min_area').value):return\\n        x,y,w,h=cv2.boundingRect(c); cx=x+w/2.0\\n        fov=math.radians(float(self.get_parameter('hfov_deg').value)); bearing=((cx/frame.shape[1])-0.5)*fov\\n        d=Detection(); d.header=msg.header; d.drone_id=self.did; d.detection_id=f'{self.did}_cv_{self.seq:06d}'; self.seq+=1\\n        d.target_class=self.cls; d.confidence=min(0.99,0.5+area/(frame.shape[0]*frame.shape[1]))\\n        # Camera-frame bearing is preserved as a simple local/world demo convention.\\n        d.position_world=Point(x=float(self.get_parameter('camera_height_m').value)*math.tan(bearing),y=0.0,z=0.0)\\n        d.covariance_xx=4.0; d.covariance_yy=4.0; d.distance_from_drone=float(self.get_parameter('camera_height_m').value)\\n        self.pub.publish(d)\\n\\ndef main():\\n    rclpy.init(); n=OpenCVDetector(); rclpy.spin(n); n.destroy_node(); ```json
{
  "type": "custom_tool_call",
  "id": "ctc_034194827118778c016ac186cea78887d08d95c212e39cb76b",
  "call_id": "call_7t8MQr9ztw7LbkfnYcAebEG4",
  "name": "exec",
  "input": "text(await tools.apply_patch(\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Add File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/tests/px4_camera_probe.py\\n+\\\"\\\"\\\"Observe actual bridged PX4 Gazebo camera frames and cv_bridge conversion.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n+import argparse\\n+import json\\n+import time\\n+\\n+from cv_bridge import CvBridge\\n+import numpy as np\\n+import rclpy\\n+from rclpy.node import Node\\n+from sensor_msgs.msg import CameraInfo, Image\\n+\\n+\\n+parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n+parser.add_argument('--output', required=True)\\n+args = parser.parse_args()\\n+rclpy.init()\\n+node = Node('px4_camera_probe')\\n+bridge = CvBridge()\\n+frames = []\\n+camera_info = []\\n+errors = []\\n+\\n+\\n+def image_cb(msg):\\n+    try:\\n+        frame = bridge.imgmsg_to_cv2(msg, desired_encoding='bgr8')\\n+        if frame.shape != (msg.height, msg.width, 3):\\n+            raise ValueError(f'converted shape {frame.shape}')\\n+        frames.append({\\n+            'stamp_ns': msg.header.stamp.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.header.stamp.nanosec,\\n+            'received_s': time.monotonic(),\\n+            'frame_id': msg.header.frame_id,\\n+            'width': msg.width, 'height': msg.height,\\n+            'encoding': msg.encoding, 'step': msg.step,\\n+            'bytes': len(msg.data),\\n+            'brightness_mean': round(float(np.mean(frame)), 2),\\n+            'brightness_std': round(float(np.std(frame)), 2),\\n+        })\\n+    except Exception as exc:\\n+        errors.append(str(exc))\\n+\\n+\\n+node.create_subscription(Image, '/drones/uav01/camera/image_raw', image_cb, 10)\\n+node.create_subscription(CameraInfo, '/drones/uav01/camera/camera_info',\\n+                         lambda msg: camera_info.append(msg), 10)\\n+start = time.monotonic()\\n+while time.monotonic() - start < 65 and len(frames) < 15 and not errors:\\n+    rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=.2)\\n+result = {'result': 'FAIL', 'failure': ''}\\n+try:\\n+    assert not errors, f'cv_bridge: {errors[0]}'\\n+    assert len(frames) >= 15, 'fewer than 15 actual image messages'\\n+    assert camera_info, 'no CameraInfo message'\\n+    assert all(f['width'] == 1280 and f['height'] == 960 for f in frames), 'wrong image resolution'\\n+    assert all(f['stamp_ns'] > 0 and f['frame_id'] for f in frames), 'missing image stamp or frame ID'\\n+    assert all(frames[i]['stamp_ns'] < frames[i + 1]['stamp_ns'] for i in range(len(frames) - 1)), 'image stamps not increasing'\\n+    assert camera_info[-1].width == 1280 and camera_info[-1].height == 960, 'CameraInfo resolution mismatch'\\n+    assert camera_info[-1].k[0] > 0 and camera_info[-1].k[4] > 0, 'CameraInfo focal lengths missing'\\n+    result['result'] = 'PASS'\\n+except AssertionError as exc:\\n+    result['failure'] = str(exc)\\n+finally:\\n+    duration = frames[-1]['received_s'] - frames[0]['received_s'] if len(frames) > 1 else 0\\n+    result.update(elapsed_s=round(time.monotonic() - start, 1),\\n+                  frame_count=len(frames),\\n+                  observed_fps=round((len(frames) - 1) / duration, 2) if duration > 0 else 0,\\n+                  image=frames[-1] if frames else None,\\n+                  camera_info={'width': camera_info[-1].width,\\n+                               'height': camera_info[-1].height,\\n+                               'frame_id': camera_info[-1].header.frame_id,\\n+                               'fx': camera_info[-1].k[0],\\n+                               'fy': camera_info[-1].k[4],\\n+                               'cx': camera_info[-1].k[2],\\n+                               'cy': camera_info[-1].k[5]} if camera_info else None,\\n+                  conversion_errors=errors)\\n+    with open(args.output, 'w') as stream:\\n+        json.dump(result, stream, indent=2)\\n+        stream.write('\\\\n')\\n+    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2), flush=True)\\n+    node.destroy_node()\\n+    rclpy.shutdown()\\n+raise SystemExit(0 if result['result'] == 'PASS' else 1)\\n*** End Patch\"));\n",
  "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {
    "turn_id": "01a103de-4599-7152-b7af-91a90b3f4b1f",
    "create_time": 1791067853.198653
  }
}

{

  "input": "text(await tools.apply_patch(\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Add File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/src/wildlife_perception/wildlife_perception/camera_geometry.py\\n+\\\"\\\"\\\"PX4 FRD/NED to Gazebo camera and ROS ENU ground-plane geometry.\\n+\\n+The pinned x500_mono_cam_down camera looks along its +X axis. Image +u is\\n+camera -Y and image +v is camera -Z. Its SDF mount is +0.10 m along body Z\\n+with +90 degrees pitch about body Y. PX4 q(w,x,y,z) maps body FRD to earth\\n+NED. The returned point is in the shared ROS ENU map frame.\\n+\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n+import math\\n+from typing import NamedTuple\\n+\\n+\\n+class GroundPoint(NamedTuple):\\n+    x: float\\n+    y: float\\n+    z: float\\n+    distance: float\\n+\\n+\\n+def _unit(q):\\n+    if len(q) != 4 or not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in q):\\n+        raise ValueError('invalid quaternion')\\n+    norm = math.sqrt(sum(v * v for v in q))\\n+    if norm < 1e-9:\\n+        raise ValueError('zero quaternion')\\n+    return tuple(v / norm for v in q)\\n+\\n+\\n+def _multiply(a, b):\\n+    ax, ay, az, aw = a\\n+    bx, by, bz, bw = b\\n+    return (aw * bx + ax * bw + ay * bz - az * by,\\n+            aw * by - ax * bz + ay * bw + az * bx,\\n+            aw * bz + ax * by - ay * bx + az * bw,\\n+            aw * bw - ax * bx - ay * by - az * bz)\\n+\\n+\\n+def _rotate(q, v):\\n+    x, y, z, w = _unit(q)\\n+    vx, vy, vz = v\\n+    tx, ty, tz = (2 * (y * vz - z * vy),\\n+                  2 * (z * vx - x * vz),\\n+                  2 * (x * vy - y * vx))\\n+    return (vx + w * tx + y * tz - z * ty,\\n+            vy + w * ty + z * tx - x * tz,\\n+            vz + w * tz + x * ty - y * tx)\\n+\\n+\\n+def px4_to_map_quaternion(q_wxyz):\\n+    \\\"\\\"\\\"Return xyzw rotation from ROS body FLU to ROS map ENU.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n+    if len(q_wxyz) != 4:\\n+        raise ValueError('PX4 attitude must be wxyz')\\n+    w, x, y, z = q_wxyz\\n+    ned_from_frd = _unit((x, y, z, w))\\n+    s = math.sqrt(.5)\\n+    enu_from_ned = (s, s, 0., 0.)\\n+    frd_from_flu = (1., 0., 0., 0.)\\n+    return _unit(_multiply(_multiply(enu_from_ned, ned_from_frd), frd_from_flu))\\n+\\n+\\n+def ground_intersection(pixel, intrinsics, uav_position, q_wxyz,\\n+                        mount_xyz=(0., 0., .10), mount_pitch=math.pi / 2,\\n+                        ground_z=0.):\\n+    \\\"\\\"\\\"Intersect an image pixel's measured-pose ray with z=ground_z.\\n+\\n+    intrinsics is (fx, fy, cx, cy). Return None if the ray is horizontal,\\n+    upward, or its intersection is behind the camera.\\n+    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n+    values = (*pixel, *intrinsics, *uav_position, *mount_xyz, mount_pitch, ground_z)\\n+    if not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in values):\\n+        raise ValueError('non-finite camera geometry')\\n+    u, v = pixel\\n+    fx, fy, cx, cy = intrinsics\\n+    if fx <= 0 or fy <= 0:\\n+        raise ValueError('camera focal lengths must be positive')\\n+    ray = (1., -(u - cx) / fx, -(v - cy) / fy)\\n+    length = math.sqrt(sum(axis * axis for axis in ray))\\n+    ray = tuple(axis / length for axis in ray)\\n+    body_from_camera = (0., math.sin(mount_pitch / 2), 0.,\\n+                        math.cos(mount_pitch / 2))\\n+    map_from_body = px4_to_map_quaternion(q_wxyz)\\n+    body_ray = _rotate(body_from_camera, ray)\\n+    map_ray = _rotate(map_from_body, body_ray)\\n+    offset = _rotate(map_from_body, mount_xyz)\\n+    camera = tuple(uav_position[i] + offset[i] for i in range(3))\\n+    if map_ray[2] >= -1e-9:\\n+        return None\\n+    distance = (ground_z - camera[2]) / map_ray[2]\\n+    if distance <= 0:\\n+        return None\\n+    return GroundPoint(camera[0] + distance * map_ray[0],\\n+                       camera[1] + distance * map_ray[1],\\n+                       ground_z, distance)\\n*** Add File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/tests/test_camera_geometry.py\\n+import math\\n+\\n+import pytest\\n+\\n+from wildlife_perception.camera_geometry import ground_intersection, px4_to_map_quaternion\\n+\\n+\\n+INTRINSICS = (500., 500., 640., 480.)\\n+LEVEL_NORTH = (1., 0., 0., 0.)  # PX4 FRD -> NED identity\\n+\\n+\\n+def test_center_pixel_uses_measured_altitude_and_map_position():\\n+    point = ground_intersection((640., 480.), INTRINSICS, (4., -3., 10.), LEVEL_NORTH)\\n+    assert point.x == pytest.approx(4.)\\n+    assert point.y == pytest.approx(-3.)\\n+    assert point.z == 0.\\n+    assert point.distance == pytest.approx(10.1)\\n+\\n+\\n+def test_left_and_right_pixels_follow_camera_and_map_axes():\\n+    right = ground_intersection((1140., 480.), INTRINSICS, (0., 0., 10.), LEVEL_NORTH)\\n+    left = ground_intersection((140., 480.), INTRINSICS, (0., 0., 10.), LEVEL_NORTH)\\n+    assert right.x == pytest.approx(10.1)\\n+    assert left.x == pytest.approx(-10.1)\\n+    assert right.y == pytest.approx(0.)\\n+\\n+\\n+def test_px4_yaw_rotates_world_ray():\\n+    yaw_east = (math.sqrt(.5), 0., 0., math.sqrt(.5))\\n+    right = ground_intersection((1140., 480.), INTRINSICS, (0., 0., 10.), yaw_east)\\n+    assert right.x == pytest.approx(0., abs=1e-6)\\n+    assert right.y == pytest.approx(-10.1)\\n+\\n+\\n+def test_camera_pitch_and_altitude_change_intersection():\\n+    point = ground_intersection((640., 480.), INTRINSICS, (0., 0., 10.),\\n+                                LEVEL_NORTH, mount_pitch=math.pi / 4)\\n+    high = ground_intersection((640., 480.), INTRINSICS, (0., 0., 20.),\\n+                               LEVEL_NORTH, mount_pitch=math.pi / 4)\\n+    assert point.y == pytest.approx(10.1)\\n+    assert high.y == pytest.approx(20.1)\\n+\\n+\\n+def test_vehicle_pitch_affects_world_intersection():\\n+    pitch = (math.cos(.1), 0., math.sin(.1), 0.)\\n+    tilted = ground_intersection((640., 480.), INTRINSICS, (0., 0., 10.), pitch)\\n+    level = ground_intersection((640., 480.), INTRINSICS, (0., 0., 10.), LEVEL_NORTH)\\n+    assert abs(tilted.y - level.y) > 1.\\n+\\n+\\n+def test_upward_and_horizontal_rays_have_no_ground_intersection():\\n+    assert ground_intersection((640., 480.), INTRINSICS, (0., 0., 10.),\\n+                               LEVEL_NORTH, mount_pitch=-math.pi / 2) is None\\n+    assert ground_intersection((640., 480.), INTRINSICS, (0., 0., 10.),\\n+                               LEVEL_NORTH, mount_pitch=0.) is None\\n+\\n+\\n+def test_frame_conversion_and_invalid_inputs():\\n+    q = px4_to_map_quaternion(LEVEL_NORTH)\\n+    assert abs(q[0]) < 1e-6 and abs(q[1]) < 1e-6\\n+    assert abs(abs(q[2]) - generate_launch_description():\\n    return LaunchDescription([\\n        DeclareLaunchArgument('sitl_enabled', default_value='false'),\\n        DeclareLaunchArgument('vehicle_count', time\\n+\\n+import cv2\\n+from cv_bridge import CvBridge\\n+import rclpy\\n+from rclpy.node import Node\\n+from rclpy.qos import qos_profile_sensor_data\\n+from sensor_msgs.msg import CameraInfo, Image\\n+from px4_msgs.msg import VehicleAttitude\\n+from wildlife_interfaces.msg import Detection, DroneState\\n+\\n+from wildlife_perception.camera_geometry import ground_intersection\\n+\\n+\\n+def orange_blob(frame, min_area):\\n+    \\\"\\\"\\\"Return (u, v, area) for the largest saturated orange shape, if any.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n+    if frame is None or frame.size == 0:\\n+        return None\\n+    hsv = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)\\n+    mask = cv2.inRange(hsv, (0, 80, 80), (35, 255, 255))\\n+    contours, _ = cv2.findContours(mask, cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)\\n+    if not contours:\\n+        return None\\n+    contour = max(contours, key=cv2.contourArea)\\n+    area = cv2.contourArea(contour)\\n+    if area < min_area:\\n+        return None\\n+    moments = cv2.moments(contour)\\n+    if moments['m00'] <= 0:\\n+        return None\\n+    return moments['m10'] / moments['m00'], moments['m01'] / moments['m00'], area\\n+\\n+\\n+class OpenCVPX4Detector(Node):\\n+    def __init__(self):\\n+        super().__init__('opencv_px4_detector')\\n+        defaults = {\\n+            'drone_id': 'uav01', 'target_class': 'dummy_animal',\\n+            'min_area': 250.0, 'max_process_hz': 5.0,\\n+            'pose_timeout_s': .5, 'attitude_timeout_s': .5,\\n+            'camera_info_timeout_s': 5.0,\\n+            'mount_x': 0.0, 'mount_y': 0.0, 'mount_z': .10,\\n+            'mount_pitch_rad': 1.5707963267948966,\\n+            'ground_z': 0.0, 'localization_sigma_m': 2.0,\\n+        }\\n+        for name, value in defaults.items():\\n+            self.declare_parameter(name, value)\\n+        self.did = str(self.get_parameter('drone_id').value)\\n+        self.bridge = CvBridge()\\n+        self.pose = self.attitude = self.info = None\\n+        self.pose_at = self.attitude_at = self.info_at = 0.\\n+        self.last_process = 0.\\n+        self.sequence = 0\\n+        self.pub = self.create_publisher(Detection, f'/drones/{self.did}/detections', 20)\\n+        self.create_subscription(DroneState, '/drones/state', self.state_cb, 50)\\n+        self.create_subscription(VehicleAttitude,\\n+                                 f'/{self.did}/fmu/out/vehicle_attitude',\\n+                                 self.attitude_cb, qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n+        self.create_subscription(CameraInfo,\\n+                                 f'/drones/{self.did}/camera/camera_info',\\n+                                 self.info_cb, 10)\\n+        self.create_subscription(Image,\\n+                                 f'/drones/{self.did}/camera/image_raw',\\n+                                 self.image_cb, 10)\\n+        self.get_logger().info(f'Real camera OpenCV detector ready for {self.did}')\\n+\\n+    def state_cb(self, msg):\\n+        if msg.drone_id == self.did:\\n+            self.pose = msg\\n+            self.pose_at = time.monotonic()\\n+\\n+    def attitude_cb(self, msg):\\n+        self.attitude = msg\\n+        self.attitude_at = time.monotonic()\\n+\\n+    def info_cb(self, msg):\\n+        self.info = msg\\n+        self.info_at = time.monotonic()\\n+\\n+    def image_cb(self, msg):\\n+        now = time.monotonic()\\n+        rate = float(self.get_parameter('max_process_hz').value)\\n+        if rate <= 0 or now - self.last_process < 1. / rate:\\n+            return\\n+        self.last_process = now\\n+        if not self.pose or self.pose.state not in (DroneState.SEARCHING, DroneState.STANDOFF):\\n+            return\\n+        if not self.attitude or not self.info:\\n+            return\\n+        for last, parameter in ((self.pose_at, 'pose_timeout_s'),\\n+                                (self.attitude_at, 'attitude_timeout_s'),\\n+                                (self.info_at, 'camera_info_timeout_s')):\\n+            if now - last > float(self.get_parameter(parameter).value):\\n+                return\\n+        try:\\n+            frame = self.bridge.imgmsg_to_cv2(msg, desired_encoding='bgr8')\\n+            found = orange_blob(frame, float(self.get_parameter('min_area').value))\\n+            if found is None:\\n+                return\\n+            u, v, area = found\\n+            info = self.info\\n+            position = self.pose.pose.pose.position\\n+            point = ground_intersection(\\n+                (u, v), (info.k[0], info.k[4], info.k[2], info.k[5]),\\n+                (position.x, position.y, position.z), self.attitude.q,\\n+                mount_xyz=tuple(float(self.get_parameter(f'mount_{axis}').value)\\n+                                for axis in 'xyz'),\\n+                mount_pitch=float(self.get_parameter('mount_pitch_rad').value),\\n+                ground_z=float(self.get_parameter('ground_z').value))\\n+        except (ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:\\n+            self.get_logger().warning(f'{self.did} camera conversion/localization: {exc}')\\n+            return\\n+        if point is None:\\n+            return\\n+        self.sequence += 1\\n+        detection = Detection()\\n+        detection.header.stamp = msg.header.stamp\\n+        detection.header.frame_id = 'map'\\n+        detection.drone_id = self.did\\n+        detection.detection_id = f'{self.did}_cv_{self.sequence:06d}'\\n+        detection.target_class = str(self.get_parameter('target_class').value)\\n+        detection.confidence = min(.99, .5 + area / (20 * float(self.get_parameter('min_area').value)))\\n+        detection.position_world.x = point.x\\n+        detection.position_world.y = point.y\\n+        detection.position_world.z = point.z\\n+ rclpy.init(args=args)\\n+    node = OpenCVPX4Detector()\\n+    try:\\n+        rclpy.spin(node)\\n+    except KeyboardInterrupt:\\n+        pass\\n+    finally:\\n+        node.destroy_node()\\n+ 'frame_id': msg.header.frame_id,\\n+                           'stamp_ns': msg.header.stamp.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.header.stamp.nanosec})\\n+\\n+\\n+def target_cb(msg):\\n+    assignments.setdefault(msg.target_id, set()).add(msg.assigned_drone_id)\\n+\\n+\\n+def phase_cb(msg):\\n+    if 'Phase=' in msg.data:\\n+        phase = msg.data.split('Phase=')[1].split()[0]\\n+        if not phases or phase != phases[-1]:\\n+            phases.append(phase)\\n+\\n+\\n+def ready_cb(msg):\\n+    global ready\\n+    ready |= msg.data\\n+\\n+\\n+def image_cb(msg):\\n+    global 30)\\n+node.create_subscription(Target, '/targets/assigned', target_cb, 20)\\n+node.create_subscription(OperatorAlert, '/operator/alerts',\\n+                         lambda msg: alerts.update({msg.drone_id: msg}), 20)\\n+node.create_subscription(String, '/mission/status', phase_cb, 10)\\n+node.create_subscription(Bool, '/drones/uav01/ready', ready_cb,
