'vehicle_status', VehicleStatus),\\n+                         status_cb, qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n+node.create_subscription(VehicleLandDetected,\\n+                         versioned_topic('/uav01',             target_id=alert.target_id, assigned_drone_id='uav01',\\n+                    decision=decision, operator_notes='V0.4 real-camera acceptance'))\\n+                if held:\\n+ print('progress', states.get('uav01').state if states.get('uav01') else None,\\n+                  'images', images, 'detections', len(detections), phases[-1:], flush=True)\\n+            node.printed = time.monotonic()\\n+    assert set(states) == {'uav01'}, 'unexpected DroneState identities'\\n+    assert states['uav01'].state == DroneState.DOCKED, 'vehicle not docked'\\n+    assert raw.get('land') and raw['land'].landed, 'PX4 landing not measured'\\n+    assert raw.get('status') and raw['status'].arming_state == VehicleStatus.ARMING_STATE_DISARMED, 'PX4 disarm not measured'\\n+    assert phases[-1:] == ['COMPLETE'], 'mission not COMPLETE'\\n+    assert ready and all(flight.values()), 'readiness/flight state missing'\\n+    assert images >= 15, 'camera images missing'\\n+ stages=sorted(stages), phases=phases, ready=ready, flight=flight,\\n+                  landed=bool(raw.get('land') and raw['land'].landed),\\n+                  disarmed=bool(raw.get('status') and V0.4 PHASE 8 — OPEN-CV REAL IMAGE DETECTION\\n==================================================\\n\\nConnect the existing or improved OpenCV detector to REAL Gazebo images.\\n\\nUse simple visual dummy targets first.\\n\\nPipeline:\\n\\nGazebo image\\n    ->\\ncv_bridge\\n    ->\\nOpenCV detector\\n    ->\\npixel detection\\n    ->\\ncamera geometry\\n    ->\\nmap/world position\\n    ->\\nexisting Detection message\\n\\nRetain the current Detection contract.\\n\\nDo not modify downstream tracker simply because the detector backend changed.\\n\\nVerify:\\n\\n- correct drone_id;\\n- detection_id;\\n- target_class;\\n- confidence;\\n- world position;\\n- distance estimate where relevant;\\n- timestamp.\\n\\n==================================================\\n16. V0.4 PHASE 9 — SIMULATED WILDLIFE TARGET MODELS\\n==================================================\\n\\nThe Gazebo world must contain suitable visual targets for camera testing.\\n\\nAdd lightweight simulated wildlife/dummy-target models.\\n\\nPriorities:\\n\\n- visually identifiable;\\n- clearly separated from terrain;\\n- easy to reproduce;\\n- suitable for OpenCV and later YOLO;\\n- academically understandable.\\n\\nDo not spend excessive effort on photorealism.\\n\\nSupport scenario-configured positions.\\n\\nInitial motion types may include:\\n\\n- stationary;\\n- straight-line;\\n- waypoint loop.\\n\\nKeep motion deterministic first.\\n\\n==================================================\\n17. V0.4 PHASE 10 — DETECTOR BACKEND ARCHITECTURE\\n==================================================\\n\\nThe perception package should support backend separation.\\n\\nConceptually:\\n\\ndetector_backend: sim\\ndetector_backend: opencv\\ndetector_backend: yolo\\n\\nThe exact architecture should follow existing code style.\\n\\nRequirements:\\n\\nSIM\\n\\n- retained;\\n- deterministic;\\n- independent of camera;\\n- remains acceptance-test backend.\\n\\nOPEN-CV\\n\\n- consumes real camera image;\\n- lightweight;\\n- useful even without neural network.\\n\\nYOLO\\n\\n- OPTIONAL;\\n- add only after camera/OpenCV pipeline is working.\\n\\nDo not make YOLO mandatory for project startup.\\n\\n==================================================\\n18. V0.4 PHASE 11 — OPTIONAL YOLO\\n==================================================\\n\\nOnly after the real camera/OpenCV path passes, optionally integrate a neural detector.\\n\\nIf Ultralytics YOLO is appropriate, isolate it in wildlife_perception.\\n\\nRequirements:\\n\\n- configurable model path;\\n- configurable confidence;\\n- configurable device;\\n- configurable classes;\\n- meaningful error if model load fails;\\n- no silent fallback pretending YOLO succeeded;\\n- existing Detection output.\\n\\nDownstream packages must not know whether a Detection came from:\\n\\n- detector_sim;\\n- OpenCV;\\n- YOLO.\\n\\nDo not remove detector_sim.\\n\\n==================================================\\n19. V0.4 PHASE 12 — TARGET TRACKING UPGRADE\\n==================================================\\n\\nReal camera detections and moving targets may expose limitations in the V0.3 tracker.\\n\\nInspect actual runtime data first.\\n\\nImprove tracking only where justified.\\n\\nPotential improvements:\\n\\n- timestamp-aware state updates;\\n- velocity estimation;\\n- constant-velocity prediction;\\n- measurement-age handling;\\n- covariance-aware gating;\\n- duplicate-track suppression;\\n- better stale-track logic.\\n\\nA simple Kalman filter is acceptable if needed.\\n\\nDo NOT add complex tracking solely because it sounds advanced.\\n\\nPreserve:\\n\\n- stable persistent target IDs;\\n- assigned UAV;\\n- operator status;\\n- target lifecycle state.\\n\\nFresh tracker updates must NOT reset an assigned target back to UNASSIGNED.\\n\\n==================================================\\n20. V0.4 PHASE 13 — MULTI-UAV SENSOR FUSION\\n==================================================\\n\\nTest two UAVs observing the same physical target.\\n\\nExpected:\\n\\nuav01 Detection\\n+\\nuav02 Detection\\n        |\\n        v\\nassociation / fusion\\n        |\\n        v\\nONE Target\\n\\nVerify:\\n\\n- target class;\\n- spatial gate;\\n- timestamps;\\n- witness UAV list;\\n- confidence;\\n- covariance;\\n- persistent target ID.\\n\\nAvoid:\\n\\ntwo cameras\\n    ->\\ntwo false independent target IDs\\n\\nif they are clearly viewing the same target.\\n\\nTest deliberately separated targets as well to ensure they are NOT wrongly merged.\\n\\n==================================================\\n21. V0.4 PHASE 14 — MOVING TARGET TRACKING\\n==================================================\\n\\nAdd a controlled moving-target scenario.\\n\\nStart simple.\\n\\nExample:\\n\\ntarget follows a rectangular or waypoint path.\\n\\nVerify:\\n\\n- persistent identity;\\n- estimated motion;\\n- association while moving;\\n- stale timeout;\\n- reacquisition;\\n- no excessive target duplication.\\n\\nIf velocity_estimate already exists in the Target interface, populate it meaningfully when justified.\\n\\n==================================================\\n22. V0.4 PHASE 15 — SWARM ASSIGNMENT ROBUSTNESS\\n==================================================\\n\\nDo not replace the existing simple assignment algorithm unless testing proves it inadequate.\\n\\nImprove robustness.\\n\\nTest:\\n\\n- one UAV / one target;\\n- two UAVs / one target;\\n- two UAVs / two targets;\\n- many UAVs / several targets;\\n- target outside maximum assignment distance;\\n- assigned UAV becomes unavailable;\\n- UAV begins RTL;\\n- UAV battery becomes low;\\n- target is lost.\\n\\nAssignment candidates should normally exclude vehicles that are:\\n\\n- unready;\\n- returning;\\n- landing;\\n- docked;\\n- faulted;\\n- below configured battery threshold.\\n\\nConsider reassignment only when operationally safe.\\n\\nKeep the algorithm understandable.\\n\\nDo NOT automatically introduce:\\n\\n- Hungarian optimization;\\n- auctions;\\n- reinforcement learning;\\n- distributed consensus.\\n\\n==================================================\\n23. V0.4 PHASE 16 — HEALTH-AWARE MISSION LOGIC\\n==================================================\\n\"}"

      "text": "{\"chunk_id\":\"0cbbab\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000013722,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":1332,\"output\":\"==================================================\\n\\nV0.3 already introduced fault reporting/recovery.\\n\\nBuild on it.\\n\\nUse actual PX4 telemetry where available.\\n\\nMonitor at mission level:\\n\\n- vehicle readiness;\\n- arming state;\\n- nav/flight mode;\\n- position validity;\\n- telemetry timeout;\\n- battery;\\n- landing state;\\n- PX4 failsafe flags where practical;\\n- command rejection/timeout.\\n\\nSafe high-level reactions may include:\\n\\nLOW BATTERY\\n    ->\\nRETURN_HOME\\n\\nTELEMETRY LOST\\n    ->\\nmark vehicle unavailable\\n    ->\\navoid new assignments\\n\\nVEHICLE FAULT\\n    ->\\nrelease its assignment if appropriate\\n\\nPX4 FAILSAFE\\n    ->\\ndo not fight PX4\\n    ->\\nreport mission-level state\\n\\nDo not recreate PX4's failsafe controller.\\n\\n==================================================\\n24. V0.4 PHASE 17 — BASIC MULTI-UAV DECONFLICTION\\n==================================================\\n\\nThis is mission-level separation, not a replacement flight controller.\\n\\nBuild on existing bounded search lanes and any V0.3 altitude separation.\\n\\nUse simple, defensible rules.\\n\\nPossible approach:\\n\\n- altitude bands;\\n- lane separation;\\n- minimum search spacing;\\n- standoff approach offset;\\n- simple predicted proximity warning.\\n\\nDo not attempt research-grade collision avoidance.\\n\\nPX4 remains responsible for low-level flight stability/safety.\\n\\nThe academic goal is to prevent obvious mission-planning conflicts.\\n\\n==================================================\\n25. V0.4 PHASE 18 — STANDOFF BEHAVIOR IMPROVEMENT\\n==================================================\\n\\nVerify real PX4 standoff flight with camera-derived target positions.\\n\\nThe aircraft should maintain safe observation distance.\\n\\nDo not approach targets unnecessarily closely.\\n\\nTest:\\n\\n- stationary target;\\n- moving target;\\n- different approach headings;\\n- CONTINUE_HOLD;\\n- target motion while holding.\\n\\nIf appropriate, update the desired standoff point as target position changes.\\n\\nKeep motion bounded and stable.\\n\\nDo not create pursuit behavior intended to harass wildlife.\\n\\n==================================================\\n26. V0.4 PHASE 19 — UGV / DEPLOYMENT REGRESSION\\n==================================================\\n\\nDo not ignore the UGV while improving UAVs.\\n\\nVerify:\\n\\nSINGLE\\n\\n- correct deployment point;\\n- intended active UAVs;\\n- readiness-aware launch.\\n\\nSPLIT\\n\\n- first deployment event;\\n- second deployment event;\\n- correct slots/UAVs;\\n- no duplicate launch;\\n- no missing second deployment.\\n\\nVerify the current waypoint-index semantics remain correct.\\n\\nDo not blindly change +1/-1 behavior unless actual code proves an error.\\n\\nVerify docking totals use configured active vehicle count.\\n\\n==================================================\\n27. V0.4 PHASE 20 — OPERATOR WORKFLOW\\n==================================================\\n\\nHuman authority remains mandatory.\\n\\nPreserve:\\n\\n/operator/decision\\n\\nand the existing typed interface.\\n\\nOperator decisions remain conceptually:\\n\\nRETURN\\nCONTINUE_HOLD\\n\\nAdditional safe operations may be exposed only if justified.\\n\\nThe operator should receive:\\n\\n- UAV;\\n- target ID;\\n- target class;\\n- confidence;\\n- target position;\\n- assigned UAV;\\n- mission state;\\n- action-required status.\\n\\nAny future GUI must call the existing typed service.\\n\\nDo not create another independent mission-control path.\\n\\n==================================================\\n28. V0.4 PHASE 21 — RVIZ IMPROVEMENT\\n==================================================\\n\\nMake the project examiner-friendly.\\n\\nUseful RViz information may include:\\n\\n- UGV;\\n- UAV locations;\\n- UAV IDs;\\n- UAV state;\\n- UAV battery;\\n- search paths;\\n- assigned search lanes;\\n- targets;\\n- target IDs;\\n- target confidence;\\n- target tracks;\\n- standoff radius;\\n- target-to-assigned-UAV line;\\n- deployment locations;\\n- optional camera field-of-view;\\n- fault/unavailable indication.\\n\\nDo not create excessive clutter.\\n\\nUse standard ROS visualization messages where possible.\\n\\n==================================================\\n29. V0.4 PHASE 22 — MISSION STATUS\\n==================================================\\n\\nEnsure the mission FSM remains correct with several vehicles.\\n\\nImportant states remain conceptually:\\n\\nINIT\\nDEPLOYING\\nSEARCHING\\nTARGET_FOUND\\nAWAITING_OPERATOR\\nRESOLVING\\nCOMPLETE\\nPAUSED\\nABORTED\\n\\nTest transitions with multiple UAVs.\\n\\nImportant:\\n\\none UAV returning must not necessarily mean entire mission is complete.\\n\\nMission COMPLETE should reflect configured mission semantics.\\n\\nTest:\\n\\n- all active UAVs complete;\\n- one faulted UAV;\\n- one returned UAV while others search;\\n- target workflow;\\n- no-target workflow;\\n- abort;\\n- pause/resume.\\n\\nDo not regress the V0.3 fix that allowed missions to leave AWAITING_OPERATOR.\\n\\n==================================================\\n30. V0.4 PHASE 23 — SCENARIO FRAMEWORK\\n==================================================\\n\\nProvide reproducible demonstrations through configuration.\\n\\nSuggested scenarios:\\n\\nA. LOGIC_ONLY\\n\\n8 logical UAVs\\n+\\ndetector_sim\\n\\nB. PX4_SINGLE_TARGET\\n\\n1 PX4 UAV\\n+\\ndetector_sim\\n\\nC. PX4_SINGLE_CAMERA\\n\\n1 PX4 UAV\\n+\\nGazebo camera\\n+\\nOpenCV\\n\\nD. self.declare_parameter('queue_depth',50); q=int(self.get_parameter('queue_depth').value); self.pub=self.create_publisher(Detection,'/detections/all',q); self.subs=[]\\n        for i in range(1,9): self.subs.append(self.create_subscription(Detection,f'/drones/uav{i:02d}/detections',self.cb,q))\\n    def cb(self,m): self.pub.publish(m)\\ndef main(args=None):\\n    rclpy.init(args=args); n=DetectionMux(); rclpy.spin(n); n.destroy_node(); rclpy.shutdown()\\nimport threading, datetime\\nimport rclpy\\nfrom rclpy.node import Node\\nfrom wildlife_interfaces.msg import Target, OperatorAlert\\nfrom wildlife_interfaces.srv import OperatorDecision\\nclass OperatorInterface(Node):\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        super().__init__('operator_interface'); self.declare_parameter('input_enabled',True); self.pending={}; self.pub=self.create_publisher(Target,'/targets/operator_decision',20); self.create_service(OperatorDecision,'/operator/decision',self.service_cb); self.create_subscription(OperatorAlert,'/operator/alerts',self.alert_cb,20)\\n        if bool(self.get_parameter('input_enabled').value): threading.Thread(target=self.keyboard_loop,daemon=True).start()\\n        self.get_logger().info('Operator interface ready: r <drone_id> = RETURN; h <drone_id> = CONTINUE HOLD')\\n    def alert_cb(self,a):\\n        if a.severity!=OperatorAlert.ACTION_REQUIRED: return\\n        self.pending[a.drone_id]=a\\n        print(\\n            f\\\"\\\\n[OPERATOR ACTION] drone={a.drone_id} target={a.target_id} \\\"\\n            f\\\"class={a.target_class} confidence={a.confidence:.0%} \\\"\\n            f\\\"position=({a.target_position.x:.1f},{a.target_position.y:.1f})\\\\n\\\"\\n            f\\\"  r {a.drone_id} -> RETURN\\\\n\\\"\\n            f\\\"  h {a.drone_id} -> CONTINUE HOLD\\\\n\\\"\\n        )\\n    def keyboard_loop(self):\\n        while rclpy.ok():\\n            try: parts=input().strip().split()\\n            except EOFError: return\\n            if len(parts)==2 and parts[0].lower() in ('r','h'): self.apply(parts[1],OperatorDecision.Request.RETURN if parts[0].lower()=='r' else OperatorDecision.Request.CONTINUE_HOLD)\\n    def service_cb(self,req,res):\\n        ok=self.apply(req.assigned_drone_id,req.decision,req.target_id); res.accepted=ok; res.message='Decision applied' if ok else 'No pending alert for drone/target'; res.timestamp=datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(); return res\\n    def apply(self,drone_id,decision,target_id=''):\\n        if decision not in (OperatorDecision.Request.RETURN, OperatorDecision.Request.CONTINUE_HOLD):\\n            return False\\n        a=self.pending.get(drone_id)\\n        if a is None or (target_id and a.target_id!=target_id): self.get_logger().warning(f'No pending alert for {drone_id} target={target_id}'); return False\\n        t=Target(); t.header.stamp=self.get_clock().now().to_msg(); t.target_id=a.target_id; t.assigned_drone_id=drone_id; t.operator_notified=True; t.status=Target.CLEARED if decision==OperatorDecision.Request.RETURN else Target.STANDOFF_HOLD; self.pub.publish(t); self.pending.pop(drone_id,None) if decision == OperatorDecision.Request.RETURN else None; self.get_logger().info(f'Operator decision {drone_id}: {\\\"RETURN\\\" if t.status==Target.CLEARED else \\\"CONTINUE HOLD\\\"}'); return True\\ndef main(args=None):\\n    rclpy.init(args=args); n=OperatorInterface(); rclpy.spin(n); n.destroy_node(); rclpy.shutdown()\\n\"}"

The Gazebo camera looks along its local +X axis;\\n+image right is camera -Y and image down is camera -Z. Its actual live\\n+CameraInfo is 1280×960 with `fx≈fy≈539.94`, `cx=640`, `cy=480`.\\n+\\n+The ROS shared map uses ENU (X east, Y north, Z up). The PX4 attitude message\\n+uses a Hamilton quaternion in `(w,x,y,z)` order rotating body FRD to earth NED.\\n+The conversion to ROS body FLU in map ENU is:\\n+\\n+```text\\n+q(ENU <- FLU) = q(ENU <- NED) * q(NED <- FRD) * q(FRD <- FLU)\\n+```\\n+\\n+`camera_geometry.py` computes a pixel ray from CameraInfo, rotates it through\\n+the SDF camera mount and the measured PX4 attitude, translates it by the\\n+current DroneState map position, and intersects the ray with a configured\\n+ground plane (`z=0` by default). Horizontal/upward rays and intersections\\n+behind the camera are rejected. The output `Detection.position_world` is map\\n+ENU, with its header stamped from the actual image and `frame_id=map`.\\n+\\n+The detector accepts position, attitude and CameraInfo only while freshly\\n+received (default receipt age limits 0.5/0.5/5 s). ROS image stamps use Gazebo\\n+simulation time, while PX4 attitude stamps are boot-relative and the existing\\n+DroneState uses the ROS node clock. These clocks have not been synchronized for\\n+exact pose interpolation. Receipt-age limits and a conservative configurable\\n+2 m localization sigma bound the demo's interpretation; they are not a claim\\n+of exact sensor-time alignment. The current camera path is validated for one\\n+PX4 vehicle and the flat ground in `wildlife_px4.sdf`.\\n+\\n+Unit checks cover center/side pixels, yaw, vehicle and camera pitch, altitude,\\n+invalid rays and PX4/ROS frame conversion. A live image-to-mission run is\\n+recorded in `docs/validation/px4_real_camera_mission.json`.\\n*** Update File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/docs/V0_4_VALIDATION.md\\n@@\\n ## Gate 7 — QGroundControl multi-vehicle view: PARTIALLY VALIDATED\\n@@\\n visually inspected in this session.\\n+\\n+## Gates 8–11 — real camera and one target mission: PASS\\n+\\n+The pinned `x500_mono_cam_down` Gazebo sensor was observed at its actual\\n+`/world/wildlife_px4/model/x500_mono_cam_down_0/.../imager/image` topic. The\\n+installed `ros_gz_bridge` published it as\\n+`/drones/uav01/camera/image_raw`, together with CameraInfo. The repeatable\\n+camera-only probe `scripts/validate_px4_camera.sh` passed: 15 RGB 1280×960\\n+frames, increasing stamps, `camera_link` frame ID, valid intrinsics, and\\n+successful `cv_bridge` conversion. Observed delivery rate was 31.18 fps for\\n+that short sample (`validation/px4_camera.json`).\\n+\\n+The world has a stationary orange visual dummy at map (15, 8) m. The\\n+`opencv` backend in `wildlife_perception` uses actual image pixels,\\n+CameraInfo, fresh DroneState position and PX4 attitude for ground-plane\\n+localization. `scripts/validate_px4_real_camera_mission.sh` passed\\n+(`validation/px4_real_camera_mission.json`, 94.8 s): 2,360 image messages,\\n+36 OpenCV Detections, one persistent Target assigned to uav01, physical\\n+standoff, operator HOLD then RETURN, measured landing/disarming and mission\\n+COMPLETE. The best measured detection was 0.38 m from the configured target\\n+position. This result does not establish a general geolocation error bound;\\n+see `V0_4_CAMERA_GEOMETRY.md` for frame and timing assumptions.\\n@@\\n-Eight-PX4 deployment/flight, QGC multi-vehicle UI inspection, camera and\\n-perception are pending validation. ```json
{
  "type": "custom_tool_call",
  "id": "ctc_034194827118778c016ac18bdf2f9887d09805343e93e9af0e",
  "call_id": "call_W1BQ7JD1VY2GthCjdPztyMas",
  "name": "exec",
  "input": "text(await tools.apply_patch(\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Delete File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/ARCHITECTURE.md\\n*** Add File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/ARCHITECTURE.md\\n+# Wildlife ROS 2 architecture — V0.4 development\\n+\\n+This continues the confirmed V0.3 nine-package workspace. Typed messages,\\n+services and actions remain unchanged. PX4 owns flight stabilization and native\\n+failsafes; ROS owns mission intent, observation, assignment, standoff requests\\n+and operator decisions. There is one PX4 adapter per real vehicle and no\\n+second mission FSM.\\n+\\n+```text\\n+wildlife_interfaces   frozen Detection, Target, DroneState and control contracts\\n+wildlife_core         shared math and RViz markers\\n+wildlife_vehicles     UGV logic, readiness-aware deployment, logical UAV, docking\\n+wildlife_perception   deterministic detector_sim; legacy logical OpenCV;\\n+                      real PX4-camera OpenCV and ray/ground geometry\\n+wildlife_targets      detection mux, tracking/fusion, persistent target registry\\n+wildlife_swarm        target-to-available-UAV assignment\\n+wildlife_mission      mission FSM and typed operator service\\n+wildlife_simulation   Gazebo worlds, UGV adapter, existing PX4 UAV adapter\\n+wildlife_bringup      launch profiles, parameters and RViz configuration\\n+```\\n+\\n+## Observation and mission flow\\n+\\n+```text\\n+Gazebo camera -> ros_gz_bridge -> sensor_msgs/Image + CameraInfo\\n+PX4 attitude --------------------\\\\\\n+PX4 local position -> PX4 adapter -> DroneState(map pose)\\n+                                   \\\\\\n+                                    OpenCV + camera geometry -> Detection(map)\\n+detector_sim -------------------------------------------> Detection(map)\\n+Detection -> detection_mux -> tracker -> Target manager -> coordinator\\n+          -> assigned Target -> existing UAV behavior -> PX4 adapter\\n+          -> namespaced PX4 command/setpoint -> PX4 -> measured telemetry\\n+                                  |\\n+                            OperatorAlert -> /operator/decision\\n+```\\n+\\n+The launch parameter `detector_backend` selects `sim`, `opencv` or `none`.\\n+The deterministic SIM backend remains the default and needs no camera. The\\n+real OpenCV profile is currently validated for uav01 only. The older V0.2\\n+OpenCV node remains available for its logical camera demo; it does not provide\\n+the V0.4 map projection. YOLO is optional and has not been added.\\n+\\n+## PX4 fleet identity\\n+\\n+Each PX4 process uses instance `i-1`, system ID and XRCE-DDS key `i`, DDS\\n+namespace `/uavNN`, and one correspondingly named ROS adapter. For the tested\\n+two-flight profile, uav01 spawns at (0, 0) m and uav02 at (0, -35) m. The\\n+adapter adds this origin to local NED-to-ENU position and subtracts it for\\n+Offboard setpoints. PX4 command targets use the matching system ID; ACKs are\\n+accepted only from the adapter's external source on that vehicle's namespaced\\n+topic. Gazebo entities are `x500_0` and `x500_1`. Eight-vehicle structural\\n+testing uses 12 m spaced columns in two rows; eight-vehicle flight has not\\n+been validated.\\n+\\n+The real camera profile mounts the pinned `x500_mono_cam_down` on uav01 and\\n+bridges its observed Gazebo image topic to\\n+`/drones/uav01/camera/image_raw`. Pixel rays use CameraInfo, PX4 FRD/NED\\n+attitude and measured map position to intersect a flat ground plane. Detailed\\n+frame definitions and timing limits are in `docs/V0_4_CAMERA_GEOMETRY.md`.\\n+\\n+## Deployment and completion\\n+\\n+The UGV requests SINGLE or SPLIT deployment. The deployment node filters\\n+active IDs, waits for recent per-vehicle readiness, and sends typed LAUNCH.\\n+The PX4 adapter queues LAUNCH until ready; flight mode, arming, position and\\n+landing are measured from PX4. RETURN delegates RTL to PX4. DOCKED means\\n+landed/disarmed at home in this simulation; it does not claim physical tray\\n+contact. The mission manager reaches COMPLETE only when the configured active\\n+vehicle count has docked.\\n+\\n+The verified V0.4 gates are listed in `docs/V0_4_VALIDATION.md`. QGC\\n+multi-vehicle UI inspection, two-camera fusion, moving visual targets and\\n+eight-vehicle flight remain separate validation tasks.\\n*** End Patch\"));\n",

  "input": "text(await tools.apply_patch(\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Update File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/ARCHITECTURE.md\\n@@\\n-# v0.2 architecture\\n-\\n-```text\\n-wildlife_interfaces\\n-        |\\n-        +--> wildlife_vehicles ---- UGV / deployment / UAV / docking\\n-        |\\n-        +--> wildlife_perception -- dummy detector / optional OpenCV detector\\n-        |             |\\n-        |             v\\n-        +--> wildlife_targets ---- mux / tracker / target manager\\n-        |             |\\n-        |             v\\n-        +--> wildlife_swarm ------- assignment / deconfliction\\n-        |             |\\n-        |             v\\n-        +--> wildlife_mission ---- mission FSM / standoff / operator service\\n-        |\\n-        +--> wildlife_core -------- visualization / math / state\\n-        |\\n-        +--> wildlife_simulation -- Gazebo assets / ros_gz bridge / adapters\\n-        |\\n-        +--> wildlife_bringup ----- Subject 1 and Subject 2 launch profiles\\n-```\\n-\\n-### External integration boundary\\n-\\n-`Gazebo -> ros_gz_bridge -> ROS 2`\\n-\\n-`PX4 SITL -> ROS 2/PX4 bridge (v0.3)`\\n-\\n-`QGroundControl -> PX4 telemetry/vehicle control (v0.3)`\\n-\\n-`Camera -> OpenCV/YOLO -> Detection`.\\n-\\n-The rest of the project consumes `Detection` and does not depend on the detector implementation.\\n+# Wildlife ROS 2 architecture — V0.4 development\\n+\\n+This continues the confirmed V0.3 nine-package workspace. Typed messages,\\n+services and actions remain unchanged. PX4 owns flight stabilization and native\\n+failsafes; ROS owns mission intent, observation, assignment, standoff requests\\n+and operator decisions. There is one PX4 adapter per real vehicle and no\\n+second mission FSM.\\n+\\n+```text\\n+wildlife_interfaces   frozen Detection, Target, DroneState and control contracts\\n+wildlife_core         shared math and RViz markers\\n+wildlife_vehicles     UGV logic, readiness-aware deployment, logical UAV, docking\\n+wildlife_perception   deterministic detector_sim; legacy logical OpenCV;\\n+                      real PX4-camera OpenCV and ray/ground geometry\\n+wildlife_targets      detection mux, tracking/fusion, persistent target registry\\n+wildlife_swarm        target-to-available-UAV assignment\\n+wildlife_mission      mission FSM and typed operator service\\n+wildlife_simulation   Gazebo worlds, UGV adapter, existing PX4 UAV adapter\\n+wildlife_bringup      launch profiles, parameters and RViz configuration\\n+```\\n+\\n+## Observation and mission flow\\n+\\n+```text\\n+Gazebo camera -> ros_gz_bridge -> sensor_msgs/Image + CameraInfo\\n+PX4 attitude --------------------\\\\\\n+PX4 local position -> PX4 adapter -> DroneState(map pose)\\n+                                   \\\\\\n+                                    OpenCV + camera geometry -> Detection(map)\\n+detector_sim -------------------------------------------> Detection(map)\\n+Detection -> detection_mux -> tracker -> Target manager -> coordinator\\n+          -> assigned Target -> existing UAV behavior -> PX4 adapter\\n+          -> namespaced PX4 command/setpoint -> PX4 -> measured telemetry\\n+                                  |\\n+                            OperatorAlert -> /operator/decision\\n+```\\n+\\n+The launch parameter `detector_backend` selects `sim`, `opencv` or `none`.\\n+The deterministic SIM backend remains the default and needs no camera. The\\n+real OpenCV profile is currently validated for uav01 only. The older V0.2\\n+OpenCV node remains available for its logical camera demo; it does not provide\\n+the V0.4 map projection. YOLO is optional and has not been added.\\n+\\n+## PX4 fleet identity\\n+\\n+Each PX4 process uses instance `i-1`, system ID and XRCE-DDS key `i`, DDS\\n+namespace `/uavNN`, and one correspondingly named ROS adapter. For the tested\\n+two-flight profile, uav01 spawns at (0, 0) m and uav02 at (0, -35) m. The\\n+adapter adds this origin to local NED-to-ENU position and subtracts it for\\n+Offboard setpoints. PX4 command targets use the matching system ID; ACKs are\\n+accepted only from the adapter's external source on that vehicle's namespaced\\n+topic. Gazebo entities are `x500_0` and `x500_1`. Eight-vehicle structural\\n+testing uses 12 m spaced columns in two rows; eight-vehicle flight has not\\n+been validated.\\n+\\n+The real camera profile mounts the pinned `x500_mono_cam_down` on uav01 and\\n+bridges its observed Gazebo image topic to\\n+`/drones/uav01/camera/image_raw`. Pixel rays use CameraInfo, PX4 FRD/NED\\n+attitude and measured map position to intersect a flat ground plane. Detailed\\n+frame definitions and timing limits are in `docs/V0_4_CAMERA_GEOMETRY.md`.\\n+\\n+## Deployment and completion\\n+\\n+The UGV requests SINGLE or SPLIT deployment. The deployment node filters\\n+active IDs, waits for recent per-vehicle readiness, and sends typed LAUNCH.\\n+The PX4 adapter queues LAUNCH until ready; flight mode, arming, position and\\n+landing are measured from PX4. RETURN delegates RTL to PX4. DOCKED means\\n+landed/disarmed at home in this simulation; it does not claim physical tray\\n+contact. The mission manager reaches COMPLETE only when the configured active\\n+vehicle count has docked.\\n+\\n+The verified V0.4 gates are listed in `docs/V0_4_VALIDATION.md`. QGC\\n+multi-vehicle UI inspection, two-camera fusion, moving visual targets and\\n+eight-vehicle flight remain separate validation tasks.\\n*** End Patch\"));\n",

{
  "type": "custom_tool_call",

  "input": "text(await tools.apply_patch(\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Add File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/tests/px4_two_camera_fusion_probe.py\\n+\\\"\\\"\\\"Two real PX4 cameras -> one persistent Target with both witnesses.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n+import argparse\\n+import json\\n+import math\\n+import time\\n+\\n+import rclpy\\n+from rclpy.node import Node\\n+from rclpy.qos import DurabilityPolicy, QoSProfile, qos_profile_sensor_data\\n+from px4_msgs.msg import VehicleLandDetected, VehicleStatus\\n+from sensor_msgs.msg import CameraInfo, Image\\n+from std_msgs.msg import Bool\\n+from wildlife_interfaces.msg import Detection, DroneCommand, DroneState, Target\\n+from wildlife_simulation.px4_uav_adapter import versioned_topic\\n+\\n+\\n+parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n+parser.add_argument('--output', required=True)\\n+args = parser.parse_args()\\n+ids = ('uav01', 'uav02')\\n+rclpy.init()\\n+node = Node('px4_two_camera_fusion_probe')\\n+states = {}\\n+histories = {did: set() for did in ids}\\n+ready = {did: False for did in ids}\\n+status = {}\\n+land = {}\\n+flight = {did: {'gcs': False, 'offboard': False, 'armed': False} for did in ids}\\n+info_counts = {did: 0 for did in ids}\\n+detections = {did: [] for did in ids}\\n+tracks = {}\\n+track_ids = set()\\n+\\n+\\n+def state_cb(msg):\\n+    if msg.drone_id in ids:\\n+        states[msg.drone_id] = msg\\n+        histories[msg.drone_id].add(msg.state)\\n+\\n+\\n+def detection_cb(msg):\\n+    if msg.drone_id in ids and len(detections[msg.drone_id]) < 100:\\n+        detections[msg.drone_id].append({\\n+            'id': msg.detection_id, 'x': msg.position_world.x,\\n+            'y': msg.position_world.y, 'confidence': msg.confidence,\\n+            'stamp_ns': msg.header.stamp.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.header.stamp.nanosec,\\n+        })\\n+\\n+\\n+def track_cb(msg):\\n+    track_ids.add(msg.target_id)\\n+    tracks[msg.target_id] = msg\\n+\\n+\\n+node.create_subscription(DroneState, '/drones/state', state_cb, 50)\\n+node.create_subscription(Detection, '/detections/all', detection_cb, 50)\\n+node.create_subscription(Target, '/targets/tracked', track_cb, 50)\\n+for did in ids:\\n+    prefix = '/' + did\\n+\\n+    def status_cb(msg, did=did):\\n+        status[did] = msg\\n+        flight[did]['gcs'] |= not msg.gcs_connection_lost\\n+        flight[did]['offboard'] |= msg.nav_state == VehicleStatus.NAVIGATION_STATE_OFFBOARD\\n+        flight[did]['armed'] |= msg.arming_state == VehicleStatus.ARMING_STATE_ARMED\\n+\\n+    node.create_subscription(VehicleStatus,\\n+                             versioned_topic(prefix, 'out', 'vehicle_status', VehicleStatus),\\n+                             status_cb, qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n+    node.create_subscription(VehicleLandDetected,\\n+                             versioned_topic(prefix, 'out', 'vehicle_land_detected', VehicleLandDetected),\\n+                             lambda msg, did=did: land.update({did: msg}), qos_profile_sensor_data)\\n+    node.create_subscription(Bool, f'/drones/{did}/ready',\\n+                             lambda msg, did=did: ready.update({did: ready[did] or msg.data}), 10)\\n+    node.create_subscription(CameraInfo, f'/drones/{did}/camera/camera_info',\\n+                             lambda msg, did=did: info_counts.update({did: info_counts[did] + 1}), 10)\\n+\\n+deploy = node.create_publisher(DroneCommand, '/deployment/commands',\\n+                               QoSProfile(depth=32, durability=DurabilityPolicy.TRANSIENT_LOCAL))\\n+mission = node.create_publisher(DroneCommand, '/mission/drone_commands', 20)\\n+\\n+\\n+def wait_for(label, condition, seconds):\\n+    deadline = time.monotonic() + seconds\\n+    while time.monotonic() < deadline:\\n+        rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=.1)\\n+        if condition():\\n+            print('PASS', label, flush=True)\\n+            return\\n+    raise TimeoutError(label)\\n+\\n+\\n+def send(pub, did, command):\\n+    pub.publish(DroneCommand(drone_id=did, command=command))\\n+\\n+\\n+start = time.monotonic()\\n+result = {'result': 'FAIL', 'failure': ''}\\n+try:\\n+    wait_for('both camera vehicles ready',\\n+             lambda: all(ready.values()) and all(did in states for did in ids)\\n+             and all(info_counts[did] > 0 for did in ids), 90)\\n+    for did in ids:\\n+        send(deploy, did, DroneCommand.LAUNCH)\\n+    wait_for('both camera vehicles searching',\\n+             lambda: all(states[did].state == DroneState.SEARCHING for did in ids), 110)\\n+    wait_for('one target with two real-camera witnesses',\\n+             lambda: any(set(track.detecting_drones) == set(ids) for track in tracks.values()), 120)\\n+    for did in ids:\\n+        send(mission, did, DroneCommand.RETURN_HOME)\\n+    wait_for('both returned and docked',\\n+             lambda: all(states[did].state == DroneState.DOCKED for did in ids), 130)\\n+    assert len(track_ids) == 1, 'same visual dummy created duplicate target identities'\\n+    assert all(detections[did] for did in ids), 'one camera did not detect the target'\\n+    for index, did in enumerate(ids, 1):\\n+        assert status[did].system_id == index, f'{did} wrong system ID'\\n+        assert all(flight[did].values()), f'{did} incomplete flight state'\\n+        assert land[did].landed, f'{did} not landed'\\n+        assert status[did].arming_state == VehicleStatus.ARMING_STATE_DISARMED, f'{did} not disarmed'\\n+        assert min(math.hypot(d['x'] - 15., d['y'] - 8.) for d in detections[did]) < 5., f'{did} no plausible map localization'\\n+        assert len(node.get_publishers_info_by_topic(f'/drones/{did}/camera/image_raw')) == 1, f'{did} missing bridged image'\\n+    assert len(node.get_publishers_info_by_topic('/drones/state')) == 2, 'DroneState publisher count'\\n+    result['result'] = 'PASS'\\n+except (AssertionError, TimeoutError) as exc:\\n+    result['failure'] = str(exc)\\n+finally:\\n+    result.update(elapsed_s=round(time.monotonic() - start, 1),\\n+                  ready=ready, info_counts=info_counts,\\n+                  states={did: states[did].state if did in states else None for did in ids},\\n+                  state_histories={did: sorted(histories[did]) for did in ids},\\n+                  flight=flight,\\n+                  detection_counts={did: len(detections[did]) for did in ids},\\n+                  minimum_target_error_m={did: round(min((math.hypot(d['x'] - 15., d['y'] - 8.) for d in detections[did])), 2) if detections[did] else None for did in ids},\\n+                  track_ids=sorted(track_ids),\\n+                  witnesses={tid: sorted(track.detecting_drones) for tid, track in tracks.items()},\\n+                  landed={did: bool(land.get(did) and land[did].landed) for did in ids},\\n+                  disarmed={did: bool(status.get(did) and status[did].arming_state == VehicleStatus.ARMING_STATE_DISARMED) for did in ids})\\n+    with open(args.output, 'w') as stream:\\n+        json.dump(result, stream, indent=2)\\n+        stream.write('\\\\n')\\n+    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2), flush=True)\\n+    node.destroy_node()\\n+    rclpy.shutdown()\\n+raise SystemExit(0 if result['result'] == 'PASS' else 1)\\n*** Add File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/scripts/validate_px4_two_camera_fusion.sh\\n+#!/usr/bin/env bash\\n+set -e\\n+source \\\"$(dirname \\\"$0\\\")/environment.sh\\\"\\n+export ROS_DOMAIN_ID=87\\n+export GZ_PARTITION=wildlife-v04-two-camera\\n+export ROS_LOG_DIR=/tmp/wildlife-v04-two-camera-logs\\n+export WILDLIFE_RUN_DIR=/tmp/wildlife-v04-two-camera\\n+cd \\\"$WILDLIFE_WORKSPACE_DIR\\\"\\n+python3 tests/px4_two_camera_fusion_probe.py \\\\\\n+  --output docs/validation/px4_two_camera_fusion.json & probe_pid=$!\\n+runtime_pid=\\n+cleanup() {\\n+  if [[ -n \\\"$runtime_pid\\\" ]]; then\\n+    kill -INT \\\"$runtime_pid\\\" 2>/dev/null || true\\n+    wait \\\"$runtime_pid\\\" 2>/dev/null || true\\n+  fi\\n+  if [[ -n \\\"$probe_pid\\\" ]]; then\\n+    kill \\\"$probe_pid\\\" 2>/dev/null || true\\n+    wait \\\"$probe_pid\\\" 2>/dev/null || true\\n+  fi\\n+}\\n+trap cleanup EXIT\\n+sleep 3\\n+HEADLESS=1 scripts/start_px4_two_camera.sh & runtime_pid=$!\\n+while kill -0 \\\"$probe_pid\\\" 2>/dev/null; do\\n+  if ! kill -0 \\\"$runtime_pid\\\" 2>/dev/null; then\\n+    echo \\\"Two-camera runtime exited; inspect $WILDLIFE_RUN_DIR\\\" >&2\\n+    exit 1\\n+  fi\\n+  sleep .5\\n+done\\n+wait \\\"$probe_pid\\\"\\n+probe_pid=\\n*** End Patch\"));\n",

[15.0, 8.0]\\n+  - [17.0, 8.0]\\n+  - [17.0, 10.0]\\n+  - [15.0, 10.0]\\n*** Add File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/src/wildlife_simulation/wildlife_simulation/target_motion.py\\n+\\\"\\\"\\\"Deterministic closed-loop waypoint interpolation for a visual dummy target.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n+import math\\n+\\n+\\n+def waypoint_position(waypoints, speed_mps, elapsed_s):\\n+    if len(waypoints) < 2 or speed_mps <= 0 or elapsed_s < 0:\\n+        raise ValueError('motion needs two waypoints, positive speed and nonnegative time')\\n+    points = [tuple(map(float, point)) for point in waypoints]\\n+    if any(len(point) != 2 or not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in point) for point in points):\\n+        raise ValueError('invalid waypoint')\\n+    segments = []\\n+    for index, start in enumerate(points):\\n+        end = points[(index + 1) % len(points)]\\n+        length = math.dist(start, end)\\n+        if length > 0:\\n+            segments.append((start, end, length))\\n+    total = sum(length for _, _, length in segments)\\n+    if total <= 0:\\n+        raise ValueError('all waypoints are the same')\\n+    along = elapsed_s * speed_mps % total\\n+    for start, end, length in segments:\\n+        if along <= length:\\n+            ratio = along / length\\n+            return (start[0] + ratio * (end[0] - start[0]),\\n+                    start[1] + ratio * (end[1] - start[1]))\\n+        along -= length\\n+    return segments[-1][1]\\n*** Add File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/scripts/move_dummy_target.py\\n+\\\"\\\"\\\"Move the Gazebo visual dummy along a YAML-configured waypoint loop.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n+import argparse\\n+import json\\n+import signal\\n+import subprocess\\n+import time\\n+\\n+import yaml\\n+\\n+from wildlife_simulation.target_motion import waypoint_position\\n+\\n+\\n+parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n+parser.add_argument('--scenario', required=True)\\n+parser.add_argument('--output', required=True)\\n+args = parser.parse_args()\\n+with open(args.scenario) as stream:\\n+    scenario = yaml.safe_load(stream)\\n+world = scenario['world']\\n+model = scenario['model']\\n+height = float(scenario['height_m'])\\n+speed = float(scenario['speed_mps'])\\n+rate = float(scenario['update_hz'])\\n+waypoints = scenario['waypoints_m']\\n+if rate <= 0:\\n+    raise ValueError('update_hz must be positive')\\n+\\n+\\n+def interrupt(signum, frame):\\n+    raise KeyboardInterrupt\\n+\\n+\\n+signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, interrupt)\\n+signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, interrupt)\\n+start = time.monotonic()\\n+accepted = 0\\n+failures = 0\\n+samples = []\\n+try:\\n+    while True:\\n+        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start\\n+        x, y = waypoint_position(waypoints, speed, elapsed)\\n+        request = (f'name: \\\"{model}\\\", position: {{x: {x:.4f}, y: {y:.4f}, '\\n+                   f'z: {height:.4f}}}, orientation: {{w: 1}}')\\n+        response = subprocess.run(\\n+            ['gz', 'service', '-s', f'/world/{world}/set_pose',\\n+             '--reqtype', 'gz.msgs.Pose', '--reptype', 'gz.msgs.Boolean',\\n+             '--timeout', '1000', '--req', request],\\n+            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=3, check=False)\\n+        if response.returncode == 0 and 'data: true' in response.stdout:\\n+            accepted += 1\\n+            if len(samples) < 100:\\n+                samples.append([round(elapsed, 2), round(x, 3), round(y, 3)])\\n+        else:\\n+            failures += 1\\n+            if failures >= 20 and accepted == 0:\\n+                raise RuntimeError('Gazebo set_pose never accepted target motion')\\n+        time.sleep(max(0., 1. / rate - (time.monotonic() - start - elapsed)))\\n+except KeyboardInterrupt:\\n+    pass\\n+finally:\\n+    result = {'result': 'PASS' if accepted > 2 else 'FAIL',\\n+              'scenario': args.scenario, 'model': model,\\n+              'accepted_updates': accepted, 'failed_updates': failures,\\n+              'samples': samples}\\n+    with open(args.output, 'w') as stream:\\n+        json.dump(result, stream, indent=2)\\n+        stream.write('\\\\n')\\n+    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2), flush=True)\\n*** Add File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/tests/test_target_motion.py\\n+import pytest\\n+\\n+from wildlife_simulation.target_motion import waypoint_position\\n+\\n+\\n+WAYPOINTS = [(0., 0.), (2., 0.), (2., 2.), (0., 2.)]\\n+\\n+\\n+def test_closed_loop_movement_is_repeatable():\\n+    assert waypoint_position(WAYPOINTS, 1., 0.) == (0., 0.)\\n+    assert waypoint_position(WAYPOINTS, 1., 1.) == (1., 0.)\\n+    assert waypoint_position(WAYPOINTS, 1., 3.) == (2., 1.)\\n+    assert waypoint_position(WAYPOINTS, 1., 8.) == (0., 0.)\\n+    assert waypoint_position(WAYPOINTS, 1., 11.) == (2., 1.)\\n+\\n+\\n+def test_invalid_motion_rejected():\\n+    with pytest.raises(ValueError):\\n+        waypoint_position([(0., 0.)], 1., 0.)\\n+    with pytest.raises(ValueError):\\n+        waypoint_position(WAYPOINTS, 0., 0.)\\n*** Update File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/src/wildlife_bringup/CMakeLists.txt\\n@@\\n   ament_add_pytest_test(opencv_px4 ../../tests/test_opencv_px4.py TIMEOUT 60)\\n+  ament_add_pytest_test(target_motion ../../tests/test_target_motion.py TIMEOUT 60)\\n*** End Patch\"));\n",

The Gazebo camera looks along its local +X axis;\nimage right is camera -Y and image down is camera -Z. Its actual live\nCameraInfo is 1280×960 with `fx≈fy≈539.94`, `cx=640`, `cy=480`.\n\nThe ROS shared map uses ENU (X east, Y north, Z up). The PX4 attitude message\nuses a Hamilton quaternion in `(w,x,y,z)` order rotating body FRD to earth NED.\nThe conversion to ROS body FLU in map ENU is:\n\n```text\nq(ENU <- FLU) = q(ENU <- NED) * q(NED <- FRD) * q(FRD <- FLU)\n```\n\n`camera_geometry.py` computes a pixel ray from CameraInfo, rotates it through\nthe SDF camera mount and the measured PX4 attitude, translates it by the\ncurrent DroneState map position, and intersects the ray with a configured\nground plane (`z=0` by default). Horizontal/upward rays and intersections\nbehind the camera are rejected. The output `Detection.position_world` is map\nENU, with its header stamped from the actual image and `frame_id=map`.\n\nThe detector accepts position, attitude and CameraInfo only while freshly\nreceived (default receipt age limits 0.5/0.5/5 s). ROS image stamps use Gazebo\nsimulation time, while PX4 attitude stamps are boot-relative and the existing\nDroneState uses the ROS node clock. These clocks have not been synchronized for\nexact pose interpolation. Receipt-age limits and a conservative configurable\n2 m localization sigma bound the demo's interpretation; they are not a claim\nof exact sensor-time alignment. The current camera path is validated for one\nPX4 vehicle and the flat ground in `wildlife_px4.sdf`.\n\nUnit checks cover center/side pixels, yaw, vehicle and camera pitch, altitude,\ninvalid rays and PX4/ROS frame conversion. A live image-to-mission run is\nrecorded in `docs/validation/px4_real_camera_mission.json`.\npx4_acceptance {'one_uav': 'PASS', 'two_uav': 'PASS', 'eight_uav': 'NOT_TESTED', 'eight_structural':
